The clawback half is covered by CI (`TestListPendingClawbackDrafts_ParkedSourceReleasedByWriteOff`); it needs a paid-prebill downgrade fixture to walk, per FLOW C2 Part B. **Re-walked 2026-08-02 through the real UI on VLX-000064**: typed-confirm dialog ("WRITE OFF"), 200 → `uncollectible`/`unknown`/seq 0, and this time the dunning-run resolution took the SIM path end to end: the invoice is clock-pinned, so its run is sim-gated out of the wall sweep (ADR-029) and resolved via a CLOCK ADVANCE — the catchup pass's terminal branch closed it `resolved/manually_resolved` with attempt_count still 0. (The handler's immediate resolve had silently no-op'd — see the false-alarm note below — so the catchup terminal branch got exercised for real.) **The re-walk also produced a false alarm worth keeping**: the run first appeared permanently wedged, ERROR-logging "get bound policy … not found" in the LIVE pass every tick. First reading — "ListDueRuns lacks a livemode filter" — was refuted by its own mutation check: RLS already scopes runs by mode under TxTenant; the wedge was the fixture itself (trigger 0021 had silently flipped a bypass-inserted run to livemode=true, creating a cross-mode row real flows cannot produce). What shipped from the detour: the property pinned as `TestListDueRuns_LivemodeIsolation` (mechanism-agnostic), and a genuine robustness reorder — processRun's terminal-resolution check now precedes the policy fetch, so an unloadable policy can never strand a terminal-invoice run (`TestProcessDueRuns_TerminalInvoiceResolvesWithoutPolicy`).)*

**Search-and-adopt (ADR-108)** — the parked invoice is no longer strictly terminal: the reconciler searches Stripe by `velox_invoice_id` metadata and acts on positive evidence only.

- [x] Park an invoice whose charge really created a PI (charge with `stripe listen` stopped so the webhook is lost; then `UPDATE invoices SET payment_status='unknown', stripe_payment_intent_id=NULL, updated_at=now()-interval '2 hours' WHERE id='<inv>'`). Within a couple of reconciler ticks the invoice **self-resolves**: log line "resolved by provider search" (succeeded PI → paid) or "adopted a live PaymentIntent" (live PI → `processing` with the id, then ordinary reconciliation finishes it). `velox_parked_invoices` drops. *(walked 2026-08-02 on VLX-000063 against REAL Stripe: finalized a $65.42 invoice for a carded (4242) customer with both `stripe listen` processes killed — inline charge minted real `pi_3TzxCC…`, then the row was rewound to the parked shape. The FIRST sweep tick (11:06:30Z, ~2 min after the PI was created — Stripe had indexed it already) logged verbatim "parked invoice resolved by provider search — succeeded PaymentIntent adopted and settled": invoice `paid`, SAME PI restamped, seq 1→2, and `paid_at` re-stamped in SIMULATED time (the customer is clock-pinned — the settle's sim binding held through adoption). Gauge 2→1 on the next tick.)*
- [x] Park an invoice with **no** real PI behind it (the pure-fabrication setup above) → after a tick, `provider_payment_status='search_not_found'` and `provider_synced_at` set; **`payment_status` still `unknown`, seq unchanged** — absence writes an observation, never a money outcome. The invoice stays parked with the write-off as the exit. *(walked 2026-08-02 on VLX-000064, same 11:06:31Z tick that resolved its sibling — the two fixtures in one sweep are each other's controls: real-PI row settled paid, no-PI row got exactly `search_not_found` + synced_at with payment_status `unknown` and seq still 0.)*
- [x] The same parked row is **not** searched again within the hour (`provider_synced_at` cool-off is the rate pacing); after an hour it rotates back in. *(walked 2026-08-02: tick #2 (~11:11Z) provably ran — it republished the parked gauge 2→1 — and VLX-000064's `provider_synced_at` stayed frozen at 11:06:31. The gauge flip is the control that makes "unchanged" mean "skipped by the cool-off predicate" rather than "no sweep happened"; the sweep summary log is activity-gated (`n>0||errs`), so silence alone proves nothing. The after-an-hour half not walked — it needs an hour; the predicate direction is CI-pinned in `TestListParkedSearchable_Predicate`.)*
- [ ] `curl -s localhost:8080/metrics | grep velox_parked_search_errors_total` — absent until an error occurs; force one (revoke the tenant Stripe key temporarily) → `class="transient"` increments and the row is stamped `search_error`. *(2026-08-02: the ABSENT half verified — two live sweeps with real searches produced no counter line, the negative control. The error half deliberately NOT walked: forcing it means breaking the Walkthrough Co Stripe config, which is unrecoverable by standing rule. The error paths are CI-pinned: `TestParkedSearch_NotOfferedDisablesOnce`, `TestParkedSearch_TransientErrorStampsAndRetries`.)*

## FLOW I5: Collect + payment timeline

- [x] Finalized unpaid → POST /v1/invoices/{id}/collect → PI created. *(walked 2026-07-27: the charge path minted real PIs on the attach-then-decline card (auto-charge sweep, same charger); no-PM → 422 "customer has no payment method set up"; post-decline collect → honest in-flight/cooldown guard message.)* *(2026-07-26: no-PM negative path verified — 422 "customer has no payment method set up"; the PI-create mechanism verified via the finalize auto-charge (same charge path, real PI). A collect-specific pending-PI fixture needs `stripe login`.)*
- [x] GET /v1/invoices/{id}/payment-timeline → all attempts in order with ts/amount/status/PI id. *(walked 2026-07-26 on NIM-000250 + NIM-000238.)*
- [x] **Coalesced rows (ADR-020)**: a paid invoice shows ONE "Invoice paid · $29.00" row, NOT a separate "Payment succeeded" row beneath it. *(walked 2026-07-26: NIM-000250 — one invoice.paid row, $605.00.)*
- [x] A voided invoice with a previously-pending PI shows ONE "Invoice voided" row, NOT a duplicate "Payment canceled" row. *(walked 2026-07-27: NIM-000253 voided with its declined PI → one invoice.voided row, zero payment_intent.canceled rows, zero resolved twins.)*
- [x] A dunning-recovered invoice shows "Invoice paid · after 3 retry attempts" — no separate "Dunning resolved" row. *(walked 2026-07-26: NIM-000241 resolved payment_recovered → one invoice.paid row with attempt_count:3, no resolved twin. NOTE: the write-off + void resolutions still show a bare "Dunning resolved" twin row — fold gap queued for the I13 walk.)*
- [x] **A failed charge renders once, from one owner (ADR-103)** — each failed charge shows as ONE row: the dunning row that carries its PaymentIntent absorbs it ("Payment retry #N attempted" with the amount and Stripe's verbatim decline reason lifted on), and a failure no campaign row owns — the initial decline — renders as its own "Payment failed" row above the "Payment recovery started" it caused. No Stripe webhook rows appear at all; the payment-failed email is its own row rather than a folded sub-line. *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000001, real decline card + three real dunning retries via clock advances: created → finalized → Payment failed $4.00 "Your card was declined." → Payment recovery started → retry #1/#2/#3 each $4.00 with the same verbatim reason → Collection paused. Pre-ADR-103 the same invoice needed four reconciliation mechanisms, two of them heuristics — a 2-minute email window and positional-index twin pairing — and the decline sorted BELOW the recovery it caused.)*
- [x] **Charged-card sub-line (ADR-020)**: paid invoice's "Invoice paid" row carries `via Visa •••• 4242` beneath the amount. *(walked 2026-07-26 on NIM-000250 saved-PM; 2026-07-27 on NIM-000248 hosted-pay — sub-line present on both.)* Holds even when the customer paid via the hosted-invoice URL **without saving the PM** (lookup goes directly to Stripe, not the local payment_methods table). Non-card PMs (bank, wallet) or Stripe lookup failures render no sub-line — graceful, not broken.
- [x] **Unpaired rows survive**: a Stripe `payment_intent.payment_failed` with no dunning twin (dunning disabled, or webhook arrived ahead of the dunning event) stays as its own "Payment failed" row. A payment-failed email row whose dispatch is still pending or failed stays visible — delivery problems must not silently disappear.
- [x] **Dunning-off failure lands in Activity at simulated time (ADR-102, reshaped by ADR-104)**: on a clock-pinned invoice whose auto-charge declined while dunning was disabled, the timeline shows "Payment failed" with the provider reason at the **simulated** instant (the charge-attempt fact) — not silence — with no duplicate webhook or email echo of the same failure. With dunning ON, rendering is unchanged (recovery row owns the failure). *(ADR-104 note: the "second card" this box once described no longer exists — there is one lane, and the attempt row now also carries a "Recorded <wall>" subline per Invariant A.)* *(walked 2026-07-28 on NIM-000270: policy disabled, 0341 decline at cycle close → Activity shows created → finalized → "Payment failed / Your card was declined. / $34.10" at Sep 1, 2026 simulated time with the lifted email sub-line; no wall-lane duplicate. Dunning-ON regression on the next cycle: one "Payment recovery started" fold, attempt suppressed. Historical NIM-000269 renders unchanged via the webhook fallback tier. Constant title + caption verified live post-#643; CI: TestApplyChargeAttemptPrecedence + TestRecordChargeAttempt_UpsertContract.)*

## FLOW I5b: Invoice attention banner

Server-derived from invoice fields. Suppressed for healthy / paid / voided / draft invoices.

### Critical
- [x] **payment_anomaly pierces terminal states** — an `amount_mismatch` or payment-received-on-VOIDED invoice shows the red anomaly banner even though the invoice is paid/voided (terminal states suppress every other banner, never this one). *(walked 2026-07-29 on VLX-000030, reached by a REAL customer action rather than a poke — see the drifted-amount box below: paying a superseded $107.25 Checkout link against an invoice that had dropped to $87.25. The invoice settled to **Paid**, and the red Critical banner still renders on it: badge `payment_anomaly`, code `payment.amount_mismatch`, message "Stripe captured 10725 (minor units) for payment pi_3TyNsmGcT3wmy5fZOAlYXmkd but this invoice booked 8725. Reconcile the difference — the recorded paid amount drives refund limits." Every other classifier is suppressed on a paid invoice; this one is not.)*
- [x] **tax_location_required**: US customer missing postal_code, finalize → **amber warning** banner badged "Customer address required" (severity corrected 2026-07-27: the classifier deliberately ranks a draft's incomplete data as warning, not the red this box previously claimed), teaching message naming postal/country/tax-ID, primary **Edit billing profile**, secondary **Retry tax**, mono code `tax.customer_data_invalid`, raw Stripe 400 under the "Provider response" disclosure. Finalize itself is BLOCKED loudly ("tax calculation pending — operator action required"). *(walked 2026-07-27 live against Stripe Tax, NIM-000260.)*
- [ ] **tax_calculation_failed (provider auth)**: revoke Stripe key → red banner code `tax.provider_auth`, action **Rotate API key**.
- [x] **payment_failed**: card decline → red banner code `payment.declined`, message = truncated `last_payment_error`, actions `[Update payment method, Retry payment]` (ADR-023). *(walked 2026-07-27 on NIM-000256 via 4000-0341 attach-then-decline — 9995 declines at SETUP too, so 0341 is the staging card; severity critical, actions exact.)* Only ONE banner — no hardcoded duplicate below the unified one. Update payment method opens the **Send setup link** dialog (emails the customer a setup link); Retry payment calls Collect.

### Warning
- [x] **tax pending**: amber banner with same code/actions, severity warning. *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000024 and VLX-000023, Walkthrough Co: a real Stripe Tax deferral at cycle close — "customer has no country on billing profile" — renders the amber banner badged "Customer address required", code `tax.customer_data_invalid`, actions **Edit billing profile** / **Retry tax** / Learn more, with a Diagnostic card carrying Tax status `pending` and the simulated "Tax deferred at" instant.)*
- [x] **payment_processing stale (ADR-049 Phase 4)**: a REAL (non-simulated) invoice left `payment_status=processing` for more than ~6h → the in-flight banner escalates Info → **amber Warning**, message points the operator at Stripe (does NOT promise auto-resolution). A clock-pinned (simulated) invoice stays **Info** no matter how "old" its sim-time is (the age is wall-clock, guarded on `!is_simulated`). *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000011, Walkthrough Co: a non-simulated invoice held at payment_status=processing with updated_at backdated 9h renders the ESCALATED copy — "Payment has been awaiting confirmation at the provider for an unusually long time — check this payment in Stripe." — with a `since` relative time and amber severity, distinct from the fresh-processing muted banner. State set directly: the box asserts RENDERING for a given age, and the >6h threshold has no product flow that produces it on demand.)*

### Info
- [x] **payment_processing (fresh)**: muted banner, **no actions**, copy says Velox confirms it automatically (true via the synchronous inline settle / reconciler backstop — ADR-049 Phases 2–3). *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000009: muted banner "Payment processing / Payment is in flight at the provider — Velox confirms it automatically when the provider responds.", code `payment.processing`, and NO action buttons.)*
- [x] **payment_unconfirmed**: muted banner, **no actions** — copy says Velox resolves it on the next reconcile. The previously-greyed-out "Check provider" button is gone (it had no endpoint; on-demand re-check deferred per ADR-049). *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000010: muted banner, code `payment.unconfirmed`, NO actions — the previously-greyed "Check provider" button is gone; copy says Velox re-checks automatically and names the 3-D Secure possibility with the Stripe next step.)*
- [x] **payment_scheduled**: `auto_charge_pending=true` → muted banner, action **Charge now**. *(re-walked 2026-07-28 with NO fixture poke: attached a real card to a card-less customer through a real Stripe Checkout setup session — the product's own recovery path — and the invoices already queued for that customer left `no_payment_method` on their own. Verified on VLX-000009 (Walkthrough Co): muted banner, code `payment.scheduled`, single action **Charge now**, clock-aware copy "Auto-charge runs on the next test-clock advance", Due-by row. Verified the promise is TRUE rather than assumed: the catchup sweep's predicate has no dunning-run filter, so it still re-charges an invoice whose recovery campaign already escalated.)*
- [x] **collection_paused**: queued invoice whose subscription is paused → muted banner, code `payment.collection_paused`, action **Charge now**. Must NOT borrow the payment_scheduled copy — the sweeps skip paused subscriptions, so nothing charges it automatically. *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000002, whose subscription dunning paused via its `pause` final action: banner reads "Collection is paused on this subscription, so this invoice will not be charged automatically. Resume collection on the subscription, or use Charge now to collect this one invoice." — and it agrees with the Diagnostic card below it ("Payment recovery · ended · 3 retries"), which is what paused it. Charge now stays offered because a manual collect is an explicit override with no pause filter. Badge reads "Collection paused" (operator copy, not the raw slug) and the reason is in the public `AttentionReason` enum. Sibling one-off VLX-000009, with no subscription to pause, still shows `payment.scheduled` — the two states separate cleanly.)*
- [x] **awaiting_payment**: muted banner, actions **Charge now** + **Email payment link**. *(re-walked 2026-07-28 — the box pins RENDERING for finalized + pending + card-on-file + `auto_charge_pending=false`, which is not a steady state any collection mode produces. Every finalize writer either charges inline or queues, so this shape means the window between the finalize commit and that write, or a crash inside it. The classifier comment claiming a "customer-pay collection mode" and a `charge_immediately_at_finalize` setting was false — neither exists in the codebase — and now names the real causes. Confirmed the two Save-&-send / Finalize paths both leave the invoice elsewhere: "Save & send" on a card-bearing customer charged immediately (VLX-000022 → paid).)*

### Banner shape
- [x] Severity styling: critical=red+AlertCircle, warning=amber+AlertTriangle, info=muted+Info. *(walked 2026-07-28 across three live invoices in one pass, reading the rendered icon element rather than eyeballing colour: VLX-000001 `payment.declined` → `lucide-circle-alert` + `text-destructive`; VLX-000025 `payment.no_payment_method` → `lucide-triangle-alert` + `text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400`; VLX-000002 `payment.collection_paused` → `lucide-info` + `text-muted-foreground`. lucide renames `AlertCircle`/`AlertTriangle` to `circle-alert`/`triangle-alert` in the DOM class — same icons.)*
- [x] Reason badge + dotted code in mono. `since` = relative time. `doc_url` → "Learn more ↗". *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000024: badge "Customer address required", `tax.customer_data_invalid` in mono under the actions, "· since 19d ago" beside the badge, "Learn more ↗" opening the doc anchor.)*
- [x] `provider_response` (raw upstream payload) → `<details>` "Provider response" disclosure. *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000024: collapsed "▸ Provider response" disclosure below the code. The `detail` half is unverified here — this deferral produced no Velox-framed detail string.)*
- [x] Healthy/paid/voided/draft → no banner. *(walked 2026-07-28 across three terminal/pre-issue states in Walkthrough Co: VLX-000012 (Paid), VLX-000018 (Voided) and VLX-000007 (Draft) each render zero attention codes and no "Learn more" — the draft showing its muted "Draft invoice — finalize to issue and begin collection." hint instead.)*

### Retry tax
- [x] **A healthy one-off invoice shows no "Tax retries" diagnostic (fixed 2026-07-26):** finalize a manual invoice with manual-provider tax → `tax_retry_count` stays **0** (the finalize-time first computation is not a retry) and the invoice page renders **no** "Diagnostic detail · Tax retries" card. Retries still count uniformly: the Retry-tax button, the background worker, and a re-finalize of a STUCK draft each bump the counter — for cycle and manual invoices alike. *(operator-reported on NIM-000258; automated + mutation-verified: `TestUpdateTaxAtomic_InitialAttemptDoesNotCountAsRetry`.)*
- [x] Banner showing → click **Retry tax** → audit log row `action='retry_tax'` with before/after attention codes. *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000023: one `retry_tax` row, `actor_type=user`, metadata carrying `before_attention: tax_location_required` → `after_attention: payment_scheduled`, `tax_status: ok`, `tax_retry_count: 1`, and `sim_effective_at` on the simulated axis — plus a separate `finalize` row from the auto-finalize. The transient "Retrying…" button label was not captured.)*
- [x] Issue fixed → invoice has `tax_status='ok'`, the tax banner disappears. *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000023: after the provider was switched back off the misconfigured one, Retry tax took the invoice to `tax_status=ok` and the tax banner was replaced by `payment.scheduled` — the invoice's next honest state. Toast text not captured.)*
- [x] Still failing → banner refreshes; each click bumps `tax_retry_count`. *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000027: with the tenant on Stripe Tax and the customer having no billing profile, Finalize was blocked and left the invoice draft at `tax_status=pending` / `customer_data_invalid` with `tax_retry_count=0` (the initial compute is not a retry). Two Retry-tax clicks without touching the cause took the count **0 → 1 → 2**, each writing its own `retry_tax` audit row carrying the count and code. The reason stayed `customer_data_invalid` because the cause was unchanged — a genuinely NEW reason needs the failure cause swapped between clicks, which this walk did not do; what is proven here is that each click really re-runs the calculation and re-persists the decision rather than no-opping.)*
- [x] Retry on non-pending/non-failed invoice → 409. *(walked 2026-07-27: saving the postal code fired the billing-profile flush which auto-retried the stuck invoice to tax_status=ok ("billing profile flush retried tax errors processed=1"), so the subsequent manual Retry-tax click hit the 409 exactly as specified.)*
- [x] **Auto-finalize (ADR-017)**: subscription-cycle invoice in `tax_status=pending` → click Retry tax with the underlying issue fixed → invoice flips to `status='finalized'` automatically (one click, not two). Status pill goes Open; hosted-invoice URL appears. *(walked 2026-07-28 end to end on VLX-000024, Walkthrough Co, with the tenant switched to Stripe Tax: the cycle close deferred on a real provider error — "customer has no country on billing profile" — and saving the fixed billing profile fired the flush, which auto-finalized it with zero Retry clicks. draft→finalized, tax_status ok.)*
- [x] **The auto-finalized invoice is actually collected afterwards** — after the flip, `auto_charge_pending=true`, and the next sweep (wall-clock tick, or Phase 3 of the next clock Advance) charges the saved card or, with no card, sends the setup-link email quoting the FINAL total. Pre-fix the invoice was finalized and owed with the flag false: invisible to both charge sweeps, to the dunning backfill (it is `pending`, not `failed`), and to dunning itself — stalled until an operator noticed. *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000024: post-finalize `auto_charge_pending=true`, and the next Advance logged "auto-charge retry: setup-link email queued" for it, then carried it on to a terminal state. While it was still a draft the log showed the new refusal — "cycle close: refusing to collect a non-finalized invoice" — and Mailpit stayed flat, confirming no pre-tax setup-link email goes out.)*
- [x] **Manual drafts stay draft**: create a manual invoice, force its tax to pending via tooling, fix the issue, click Retry tax → tax becomes ok BUT invoice stays draft (operator must finalize explicitly). *(walked 2026-07-27: NIM-000260 stayed draft through the flush-driven recompute; explicit finalize then committed real Stripe Tax — tax_1TxYyU…. A NY-address twin proved nonzero end-to-end: NIM-000261, $8.88 at 8.875% committed. The earlier CA $0 was CORRECT — CA does not tax the tenant's SaaS tax code.)* Toast: "Tax recalculated successfully".

### Background tax-retry reconciler (ADR-017)

> **Re-assessed 2026-07-29 (the 07-29 morning blocked-note was wrong twice, and walking past it found a real bug).** (1) A retryable failure IS reachable without touching Walkthrough's Stripe: a scratch tenant with `tax_provider=stripe_tax` saved but Stripe never connected produces `provider_not_configured` — retryable per `TaxRetryableErrorCodes()` — on every cycle-close finalize. Two such tenants exist (TaxRetry Co, TaxLadder Co). (2) The real schedule is `[5m, 15m, 1h, 4h, 12h, 24h, 48h, 96h]` — full exhaustion ≈ **7.7 days**, not hours; the ladder tenant is left running to walk box 3 for real. (3) **The walk found the reconciler ignoring its own single source**: both scan call sites hardcoded `{provider_outage, unknown}` against `TaxRetryableErrorCodes()`'s three codes, so `provider_not_configured` rows NEVER entered the scan — twelve live ticks at `retry_count=0` while the attention banner promised "calculation will retry on the next scheduler tick." Fixed same day (both sites now read the domain list; CI-locked by `TestRetryPendingTax_ScansTheDeclaredSingleSource`, which seeds one stuck invoice per retryable code).
- [x] Force a subscription-cycle invoice into `tax_pending` with a retryable code → reconciler retries within one tick; resolved cause → auto-finalizes. *(FULLY walked 2026-07-29 on TaxRetry Co VLX-000001, all real flow: `provider_not_configured` deferral from a genuine cycle prebill sat frozen through 12 pre-fix ticks (the #661 bug), then post-fix climbed the cron ladder (retries 1→3, stamps +5m/+15m/+1h). Recovery leg: operator connected Stripe (real pk+sk) → ADR-019 provider-config flush fired and the error honestly ADVANCED to `customer_data_invalid` (address truly missing) → billing-profile save fired the sibling customer-data flush → tax computed against REAL Stripe Tax (0% — account has no CA registration; an honest provider answer) → **draft→finalized, tax_status=ok, retry_count=6, zero manual retry clicks**. The two flush siblings chained exactly as ADR-019 designed.)*
- [x] Same shape with `tax_error_code='customer_data_invalid'` → reconciler does NOT touch it (non-retryable code). Manual operator action still required. *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000023: a real cycle-close deferral sat at `customer_data_invalid` for ~20 minutes — four scheduler ticks at the local 5-minute interval — with `tax_retry_count` still **0**. It only moved when an operator acted, and the code is absent from `TaxRetryableErrorCodes()`. The sibling VLX-000024 moved on a *billing-profile save*, which fires the per-customer flush — not the reconciler.)*
- [ ] After 8 attempts the row exits the reconciler scan: *(ARMED 2026-07-29: TaxLadder Co VLX-000001 climbing the real ladder, never to be connected — expected exhaustion ≈ 2026-08-06; tick this box from live state then.)* `psql -tAc "SELECT id, tax_retry_count, tax_next_retry_at FROM invoices WHERE id='vlx_inv_xxx';"` shows `tax_retry_count=8`, `tax_next_retry_at=NULL`. Banner stays live for the operator; worker stops.
- [x] Backoff respected: the 1st retry is ~5min ahead (±10% jitter), the 5th ~12h ahead (schedule `[5m, 15m, 1h, 4h, 12h, 24h, 48h, 96h]`). Sub-5-min ticks don't double-process the row. *(FULLY walked 2026-07-29 on TaxLadder Co VLX-000001, all five claimed rungs observed live: retry #1 stamped +5m01s, #2 +14m20s/+15m05s (±10% jitter shown across the two tenants), #3 +1h, #4 +4h, #5 +12h (next_attempt 2026-07-30T03:22Z — surfaced verbatim on the banner as "Engine will retry: <t>", the plumbed tax_next_retry_at, never a fabricated cadence). No-double-process observed directly: the 11:14:29 tick correctly skipped rows due at 11:14:36/39. Verification pass rode along: State-1 banner shows "Connect Stripe" action copy with NO tick promise; a LIVE-mode invoice on the test-connected tenant correctly reads "Connect Stripe … for the active mode" (StripeConnected is per-mode — walked on a real live-partition deferral, retries=1 from the live cron); exhaustion copy (critical severity, promises dropped, manual Retry now) is code-verified and gets its live confirmation with the ~Aug 6 exhaustion box.)*
- [x] **Tax-commit reconciler recovers a lost transaction id** — on a finalized `stripe_tax` invoice, NULL the `tax_transaction_id` to simulate the commit-succeeded-but-persist-failed orphan (no product flow can make Velox lose a write, so this is the one step that must be a direct DB write). Within one scheduler tick it is repopulated with the **same** `tx_*`, proving the idempotency key returned the ORIGINAL transaction rather than minting a duplicate. A `manual`-provider invoice is never picked up. *(walked 2026-07-29 on VLX-000028 — a real Stripe Tax invoice at 8.875% US-NY, $200.00 + $17.75, `is_simulated=false`: transaction `tax_1TyNUlGcT3wmy5fZuvbbfOOE` nulled at 02:46:04Z, and the 02:47:43Z tick logged `reconciler swept · reconciler=tax_commit · advanced=1 · errors=0` with the id restored **byte-identical**. Manual-provider VLX-000025 stayed at (null) throughout — the predicate needs `tax_provider='stripe_tax'` AND a non-empty `tax_calculation_id`. Two corrections: the log line is `reconciler swept`, not the `tax commit recoveries … recovered=1` this box quoted; and the scan also excludes `is_simulated` rows, so a clock-pinned invoice like VLX-000024 is deliberately never recovered by the wall-clock worker.)*

### List + draft cleanup
- [x] `/invoices` rows: severity-tinted dot next to invoice number; tooltip surfaces typed reason. Healthy/draft = no dot. *(walked 2026-07-28 across a 11-row list covering every state: destructive dot for payment_failed ("The customer's card was declined."), amber for stale-processing and dunning_exhausted, blue for fresh processing / unconfirmed — each tooltip carries the TYPED reason + operator copy — and NO dot at all on paid, voided, or draft rows.)*
- [x] Draft rows show "draft" pill (Dashboard) or em dash (Invoices, Subscription detail) instead of `payment_status='pending'`. *(walked 2026-07-28: VLX-000007 renders `draft | —` on /invoices — the em dash, never `pending`.)*
- [x] Invoice detail draft row: muted "Draft invoice — finalize to issue and begin collection." hint. *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000007: the muted hint reads verbatim "Draft invoice — finalize to issue and begin collection." and NO attention banner renders on a draft.)*
- [x] **No-payment-method nudge (customer WITH email) resends the SETUP link, not the invoice email** *(walked 2026-07-26 on NIM-000253: present-tense card copy, code payment.no_payment_method, Resend setup link → new "Set up payment for invoice NIM-000253" Mailpit email, no recipient dialog.)* — on that finalized-unpaid no-card invoice for a customer that **has an email on file**, the attention card states the behavior in present tense (*"Velox emails the customer a setup link when an invoice finalizes…"* — never a past-tense send it can't prove) and its button reads **"Resend setup link"**. Click it → a "Setup link resent" toast (no recipient dialog) and Mailpit shows another **"Set up payment"** email (`POST /v1/invoices/{id}/resend-setup-link`) — the same Checkout-setup link, NOT the hosted-invoice "pay this invoice" email. For an invoice in a different attention state (e.g. `payment_failed`), the `send_reminder` button still opens the email dialog and sends the hosted-invoice pay link.
- [x] **No-payment-method nudge (customer with NO email) shows the honest variant** *(walked 2026-07-28 on NIM-000268: email-less clock-pinned customer, arrears cycle close → payment.no_payment_method; banner copy verbatim incl. "or add an email so it can be sent"; only Open customer page + Learn more — no Resend setup link; dialog's Send email disabled with "No email on file" placeholder; Copy link minted a real cs_test_… Checkout setup URL.)* — repeat on a finalized-unpaid no-card invoice for a customer with **no email address on file** (create one without an email, or clear it). The card now reads *"…We email a setup link only when the customer has an email address on file — open the customer page to copy a secure setup link…"* and offers **only "Open customer page"** — the **"Resend setup link" button is gone** (a resend can't send with no recipient). Open the customer page → **Add payment method → Copy link** mints a setup URL to hand to the customer directly.
- [x] **Clock-aware auto-charge tail** — the no-PM banner on a test-clock invoice ends *"…auto-charges this invoice on the next test-clock advance"*; on a wall-clock invoice, *"…on the next billing tick — no operator action needed"*. No second client-rendered copy of the promise below the banner. *(walked 2026-07-22 on NIM-000234 live: "next test-clock advance" tail + no duplicate line.)*
- [x] **Diagnostic detail agrees with the banner on a card-less invoice** — the run row reads "Payment recovery · No retries yet" (or "N retries", never "attempt 0"), and the date row is **"Next reminder"** with muted "Won't charge until a card is attached." Decline (has-card) dunning keeps "Next retry". *(walked 2026-07-22: NIM-000234 "No retries yet"/"Next reminder"; NIM-000214 "ended · 3 retries".)*
- [x] **No dead Collect button** — with no PM on file the disabled "Collect Payment" primary is absent; it returns once a card is attached. *(walked 2026-07-22 on NIM-000234: disabled primary gone, banner actions only.)*
- [x] **dunning_exhausted** — let a no-PM run exhaust (final action fires, run `state=escalated`) → banner becomes *"Payment recovery has ended without collecting…"* (code `payment.dunning_exhausted`, label "Payment recovery ended"), still offering Resend setup link (with email) / Open customer page; Diagnostic badge reads **ended** with no future-step row. Settling the invoice clears it. *(walked 2026-07-23 on NIM-000214 live: banner + code + "ended" badge, no future-step row.)*

- [x] **Uncollectible invoice's hosted page is honest** — a `mark_uncollectible` dunning outcome's escalation email links to a hosted page that offers NO Pay button and says so. *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000017: escalation email CTA reads **"View invoice"** (not a Pay CTA that would dead-end) and the page renders "This invoice is closed / Online payment is no longer available for this invoice. Please contact support to settle it or restore service." THIS WALK FOUND THE EMAIL WAS NEVER SENT AT ALL: the outbox staleness gate treated `uncollectible` as "settled", and mark_uncollectible marks the invoice in the same breath as it enqueues the escalation — so the one email whose purpose IS that outcome was skipped every time, and the template's uncollectible branch was unreachable in production. Fixed: the gate now asks WHICH terminal state, and an escalation announcing an uncollectible invoice delivers; paid/voided still mute it, and the other action-required types stay muted on uncollectible.)*

Registered as an enhancement with a trigger rather than silently dropped; the PDF attachment and hosted page both carry the lines today. A zero-due invoice sends the "— no payment due" variant instead.)*

We'll try again on August 7, 2026" with a "Pay invoice" CTA; the mark_uncollectible escalation (VLX-000017) renders "View invoice". DOC CORRECTED: the box previously said the escalation CTA is "Resolve invoice" — no such label exists; the template deliberately switches on whether the invoice is still payable so the CTA never dead-ends.)*

## FLOW I8: Currency coherence (ADR-100 guard ring)

- [x] Republish a plan-bound price in a different currency → 409 naming the plan(s); same-currency rate change → new version OK. Archive the (sub-less) plan → the different-currency republish now passes (the wrong-currency repair flow). *(CI-locked `TestCurrencyRing_RepublishBlockedWhilePlanBound` / `ArchivedPlanRepairFlow`, real-Postgres, green 2026-07-26.)*
- [x] Create an EUR plan wiring a USD-ruled meter → 409 naming the price key; same for ATTACHING the meter via plan update, for binding an EUR price onto a USD meter (pricing-rule row or default binding), and for a JPY/KRW/VND/CLP currency anywhere (zero-decimal: refused loud with the minor-unit explanation — Stripe would read the cents as whole units, a 100× charge). *(CI-locked `TestCurrencyRing_*`.)*
- [x] Customer currency pin: set `billing_profile.currency=EUR` first → creating a USD-plan sub 409s (ordering bypass closed); with an EUR sub live, a USD second sub 409s naming it; a cross-currency plan swap 409s even on a single-item sub (raw-cents proration); a draft that diverged before the pin refuses at Activate. Coherent twins all pass. *(CI-locked `TestCurrencyPin`, real-Postgres.)*
- [x] **One-off invoices follow the customer pin by default (2026-07-26):** `POST /v1/invoices` with no explicit currency resolves billing-profile currency → tenant default → USD — a GBP-pinned customer's bare API create previously minted a silent USD invoice (the composer masked it by sending its picker value). Explicit input still wins (one-off charges may deliberately denominate differently — they join no proration math) and still hits the zero-decimal 422. *(found live on i8-gbp-cust; automated: `TestCreate_CurrencyResolvesProfileThenTenantDefault`.)*
- [x] Second AI recipe in a different currency → 409 at meter adoption naming the conflicting price ("a meter's prices share one currency"); same-currency install adopts fine. *(CI-locked `TestService_Instantiate_RefusesCrossCurrencyMeterAdoption`.)*
- [x] Invoice-header precedence: **billing profile > first item's plan > tenant settings** (that last one a dead branch — plans always carry currency) > USD. Setting a currency never re-labels invoices that already exist. *(walked 2026-07-29 on D4 Self Serve, a sub-less customer whose invoices were all USD under the tenant default: setting the billing profile's Currency to EUR made the very next invoice (VLX-000033) come out **EUR**, and the composer states its own provenance — "€ EUR — Euro / **From customer billing profile.**" — so the operator can see which tier won rather than guessing. Non-retroactivity held on all three older invoices, including the **draft** VLX-000007, which is the interesting one: a mutable row was still left at USD rather than re-stamped. Walked the profile-over-default tier only; the plan tier isn't separately observable here because every plan in this tenant is USD, and the tenant-settings tier is the dead branch the box itself names.)*
- [x] Webhook settle in a currency ≠ invoice header → `payment.currency_mismatch` anomaly on the invoice (report-only tripwire; case-folded). *(walked 2026-07-29 with a real EUR charge against a USD invoice: `pi_3TyQB7…` created at Stripe for **eur** 900 with VLX-000010's metadata. Log: "payment anomaly: charge settled in EUR but the invoice is labeled USD"; banner: **"Payment pi_3TyQB7… was charged in a different currency than this invoice shows. The amounts were not converted — review the charge in Stripe and correct the invoice or refund."** **Report-only confirmed** — the invoice still settled to Paid with amount_paid 900; the tripwire flags, it does not block. **Case-folding confirmed by a negative**: a lowercase `usd` PI against the `USD`-labelled VLX-000009 settled to Paid with anomaly **(none)** — which is also why every ordinary settle in the system is clean, since Stripe always sends lowercase currency codes.)*

Download the CN PDF: line amount **$110.00**, totals rows read **"Total excluding tax" $100.00**, tax row **$10.00**, **"Credit Total" $110.00** — line amounts sum to Credit Total; no row claims to be a sum-of-lines "Subtotal" that doesn't match. *(walked 2026-07-26: CN-000032 on NIM-000250 — line $605.00 gross, "Total excluding tax" $550.00, "Sales Tax (10%)" $55.00, "Credit Total" $605.00, PDF verified visually.)*
