A failed number allocation FAILS the Create loudly; no CN with a timestamp-shaped number (`CN-YYYYMM-…`) is ever created. *(walked 2026-07-28 on Walkthrough Co, which had zero credit notes: two real issues against VLX-000014 came out `CN-000001` then `CN-000002`, and the invoice's amount due fell $60.00 → $45.00 as the $10 + $5 landed. Both allocation sites in `creditnote/service.go` hard-fail on an allocator error OR an empty number with no synthesized fallback — the comment there names the removed `CN-YYYYMM-<unixnano%1e6>` scheme and why (non-monotonic, collision-prone, and it hid the misconfiguration) — and `SELECT count(*) … WHERE credit_note_number ~ 'CN-[0-9]{6}-'` is **0** across every tenant in the database.)*

## FLOW TR-CXL: Trial cancellation (ADR-069)

- [x] Trialing sub → Cancel dialog first option reads **"At trial end"** with "won't be charged" copy; confirm → banner shows "at trial end (<trial_end date>)", GET returns `cancel_effective_at == trial_end_at`. *(walked 2026-07-29 on a fresh clock "TR-CXL walk" parked Aug 1 2027, sub TR-CXL cancel (14d trial → Aug 15): dialog's DEFAULT-selected first option reads "At trial end / Trial runs until Aug 15, 2027, then the subscription cancels — the customer won't be charged. Reversible until then."; second is "Immediately / Stops billing right now. Cannot be undone." After Schedule cancellation the banner reads "Cancellation scheduled — at trial end (Aug 15, 2027, 9:00 AM UTC); the customer won't be charged" and GET returns `cancel_effective_at == trial_end_at == 2027-08-15T09:00:00Z`.)*
- [x] Advance the test clock past trial end → sub is **canceled**, `canceled_at == trial_end_at`, invoice list shows **NO invoice**, timeline shows one cancellation entry, webhook log shows one `subscription.canceled` (reason `trial_end_cancel`) and **zero** `subscription.trial_ended`. *(walked 2026-07-29: advanced Aug 1 → Aug 16 2027 in one click; sub is `canceled` with `canceled_at = 2027-08-15T09:00:00Z` exactly == trial_end_at (not the advance target), customer has **0 invoices** — the free-cancel guard held — and webhook_outbox carries exactly `subscription.created` → `subscription.cancel_scheduled` → `subscription.canceled` (reason `trial_end_cancel`), with `subscription.trial_ended` count = **0**.)*
- [x] **Lifecycle events ride the transition tx (2026-07-05):** `SELECT event_type, payload FROM webhook_outbox` right after any create/activate/cancel/end-trial → `subscription.created` / `.activated` / `.canceled` / `.trial_ended` rows exist **before** any dispatcher tick (enqueued IN the transition tx, exactly once per transition; a rolled-back cancel leaves no row). Payload keeps the provenance fields (`canceled_by`, `reason`, `triggered_by`). *(automated: `TestLifecycleEvents_EnqueuedInTransitionTx`)*
- [x] Schedule the cancel, then **Undo** before trial end, advance the clock → sub ACTIVATES normally and bills period 1 (the rescind won). *(walked 2026-07-29 on a second sub under the same clock: `POST /schedule-cancel {cancel_at: trial_end}` → `cancel_effective_at` set; `DELETE /scheduled-cancel` → cleared to null, still `trialing`; after the same Aug 16 advance the sub is **active** on period Aug 15 → Sep 15 2027 with invoice VLX-000040 finalized at $42.90 — the rescind won and period 1 billed. NOTE: `POST /{id}/cancel` is the IMMEDIATE cancel; scheduling is `POST /{id}/schedule-cancel` and undo is `DELETE /{id}/scheduled-cancel`.)*
- [x] Schedule, then **Extend trial** with an explicit cancel date pending → 409 "clear the scheduled cancel first"; with the flag-only schedule → extension succeeds and the banner's date moves to the new trial end. *(walked 2026-07-29 on TR-CXL negatives: explicit `cancel_at=trial_end` pending → extend-trial 409 `invalid_state` "subscription has a scheduled cancellation with an explicit date — clear the scheduled cancel first, then extend the trial". Cleared, re-scheduled flag-only (`at_period_end:true`) → extend to Sep 6 returned 200, trial_end_at moved, AND `cancel_effective_at` followed to Sep 6 — the flag tracks the moving boundary, which is exactly why the explicit-date variant must refuse. Banner reads "Cancellation scheduled — at trial end (Sep 6, 2027, 9:00 AM UTC); the customer won't be charged". API field is `trial_end`, not `trial_end_at`.)*
- [x] "End trial now" is disabled (with reason) while a cancel is scheduled; via API → 409. *(walked 2026-07-29: button `disabled=true` with tooltip "Clear the scheduled cancellation first"; `POST /end-trial` → 409 "subscription has a scheduled cancellation — clear the scheduled cancel first, then end the trial".)*
- [x] Explicit `cancel_at` strictly between trial end and period end → **422** (validation_error — the repo-wide validation status; this box previously said 400) naming both valid boundaries. *(walked 2026-07-29: `cancel_at=2027-09-05` on a sub with trial_end Aug 30 / period_end Sep 30 → 422 "for a trialing subscription cancel_at must be the trial end (2027-08-30T09:00:00Z) or on/after current_billing_period_end (2027-09-30T00:00:00Z) — dates in between are honored by neither boundary".)*
- [x] Immediately cancel a trialing sub whose trial just elapsed (scheduler hasn't activated it) → NO final invoice. *(walked 2026-08-02 on a fresh scratch tenant, TrialWalk Co — Walkthrough Co's owner credential was rotated by a concurrent session mid-day, and this box needs no Stripe. Fixture per strategy §3.13 (tick-phase): wall-clock sub `vlx_sub_d9nn7n3…` on a $29/mo 14-day-trial plan; the 16:56:24Z scheduler tick was observed in the log, then `trial_end_at` backdated to now()−60s at 16:56:35 — the ONLY poke, the premise axis; status verified still `trialing` with the trial elapsed. `POST /{id}/cancel` at 16:56:49 → 200, `canceled`, `canceled_at=16:56:52` (the cancel instant — the immediate path does NOT stamp trial_end_at; only the scheduled-at-trial-end path does). Customer invoice count **0**; webhook_outbox carries exactly `subscription.created` → `subscription.canceled` (`canceled_by=operator`, enqueued in the transition tx), zero `.activated` / `.trial_ended`. The negative control the box exists for: the NEXT tick (17:01:24) ran against this elapsed-trial canceled sub and wrote nothing — still canceled, still 0 invoices, outbox still 2 rows. The free-cancel guard holds through the race window, in both directions of the tick.)*

## FLOW C-ARCH: Archive semantics (ADR-067)

- [ ] Customer with an ACTIVE subscription → Archive → 409 toast naming the subscription; customer stays active. Same for trialing and scheduled-cancel subs.
- [ ] Cancel the subscription(s) → Archive succeeds; customer hidden from active list; unpaid invoices remain payable and dunning reminders continue.
- [ ] Archived customer: `POST /v1/subscriptions` → 409 "customer is archived". Unarchive → create succeeds.
- [ ] Archived plan: create/swap onto it → 409; existing subs on the plan keep billing.
- [ ] Billing profile: set currency `EUR` while an active sub's plan is USD → 409 explaining the re-denomination; `usd` (lowercase) on a USD plan → saves as `USD`; `EURO` → 400.

## FLOW I10: Hosted invoice page

- [x] **Test-mode banner (2026-07-05):** open a hosted invoice minted from a **test-mode** key *(walked 2026-07-27 on NIM-000248: amber banner verbatim; payload livemode:false)* → amber "Test mode — this invoice is a test and no real payment will be collected." banner above the status banner; the payload carries `livemode: false`. A live-mode invoice shows no banner. The public cost-dashboard JSON (`GET /v1/public/cost-dashboard/{token}`) also carries `livemode` for embed banners.
- [x] **One live payment link per invoice (ADR-068):** click Pay twice quickly (or from two browsers) → both land on the SAME Stripe session URL; pay it once → the invoice settles once. `checkout_sessions` shows one open row flipping to `invoice_settled` on payment. *(walked 2026-07-27: two Pay clicks → identical cs_test_a1fnLO… session; paid once; claim row → invoice_settled; the settle's best-effort expire of the completed session WARNs harmlessly per the ExpiresAt-backstop design.)*
- [x] **Stale session dies on settle/void/credit** — open the Pay page (don't pay), then void the invoice → the claim row closes in the same operation, a new `POST /checkout` 409s (`not_payable`), and the Stripe session is expired. *(walked 2026-07-29 on VLX-000029, a $50 one-off: Pay minted `cs_test_a17x0keo…` and the row read `open`. Void (typed `VOID` confirm) → invoice `voided` and the same row flipped to **expired** in the same operation — no sweep needed. Verified at the provider, not just locally: `stripe checkout sessions retrieve` reports `status=expired, payment_status=unpaid`. The hosted page then drops the Pay button entirely and states "Voided on Jul 29, 2026 — this invoice is no longer owed.", and `POST /v1/public/invoices/{token}/checkout` returns **409 `not_payable`** — "invoice is not in a payable state". Only the void arm was exercised; the settle and covering-credit arms of this box are still unwalked.)*
- [x] **Drifted amount never mints blind** — open a session, apply a partial credit note (amount_due drops), click Pay again → a NEW session at the new amount, old superseded. If the customer completes the old session the settle raises `payment.amount_mismatch` with a Critical banner. *(walked 2026-07-29 on VLX-000030 at $107.25: Pay minted session A (`cs_test_a15DHy…`, 10725). A $20 credit note dropped amount_due to $87.25 and flipped A to **superseded** immediately. Pay again minted session B at **8725** — the button itself reads "Pay $87.25". Then the adversarial half: A is deliberately NOT expired at Stripe on supersede (it still offered $107.25), because the expire sweep runs at SETTLE time — `ListUnresolvedForInvoice`'s comment states this, and Stripe's own ~1h expiry is the other bound. Paying that stale link with 4242 settled the invoice **Paid** with amount_paid 8725 against a 10725 capture, stamped `payment.amount_mismatch`, and the settle sweep expired sibling session B. So the stale link is not blocked, it is *caught* — and loudly. Not walked: the `payment_in_progress` 409, which needs the narrow window between completing the old session and the settle landing.)*
- [x] **Duplicate charge is loud** — a second different-PI success on a paid invoice raises the Critical "second payment succeeded" banner naming both PIs; `payment.duplicate_charge` fires; same-PI redeliveries stay silent. *(walked 2026-07-29 with a GENUINE Stripe charge, not a synthetic payload: created a real second PaymentIntent at Stripe (`pi_3TyQ9G…`, $7.00, `pm_card_visa`) carrying VLX-000021's `velox_invoice_id` metadata, and let the real `payment_intent.succeeded` ride the running `stripe listen` forwarder through the real handler. Banner: **"A second payment (pi_3TyQ9G…) succeeded on this invoice after it was already paid (original payment pi_3TyD4L…). The extra charge exists only in Stripe — review and refund it."** — code `payment.duplicate_charge`, rendering on a **Paid** invoice. Critically the invoice did NOT absorb the duplicate: `stripe_payment_intent_id` stayed on the original and `amount_paid_cents` stayed 700 rather than doubling. Same-PI redelivery negative walked too — `stripe events resend` of that same event left the webhook table at 98 rows (the `(tenant_id, stripe_event_id)` UNIQUE dedup), fired no second anomaly, and changed nothing on the invoice.)*
- [x] **Post-payment settle poll (ADR-067 companion):** pay via the hosted page → on the `?paid=1` redirect the page shows "Processing your payment…" with NO Pay button, then flips to Paid within a few seconds without a manual refresh. Simulate a failed charge → red "Payment didn't complete — your card was not charged" banner and Pay returns. Stall the webhook >3 min → amber "taking longer than usual — you will not be charged twice" copy, Pay still hidden. *(walked 2026-07-28 by killing `stripe listen` before paying: NIM-000265 showed the green Processing interim with no Pay button, then the amber "Confirmation is taking longer than usual — you will not be charged twice" copy after the 3-min poll cap; restarting the forwarder + `stripe events resend` settled it to paid. Failed-charge leg on NIM-000266: red banner + Pay returned.)*

- [x] Draft invoice has no `public_token`. Finalize → token minted (`vlx_pinv_` + 64 hex). *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000018: the draft's create response carries public_token=null; finalize mints `vlx_pinv_` + exactly 64 lowercase hex.)*
- [x] Detail page: public-link actions live in the **More invoice actions** overflow menu as **Copy public link** / **Rotate public link** (this box said a bare "Copy Link" button until 2026-07-28). **Rotate** opens a typed-confirm dialog (type `ROTATE`). Both hidden on draft. *(walked 2026-07-28: on finalized VLX-000025 the menu carries both, and Copy public link really does put `http://localhost:5173/invoice/vlx_pinv_…` on the clipboard — followed it to a live hosted page. Rotate's dialog explains the blast radius ("Any email, message, or page still linking to the old URL will stop working") and its **Rotate Link** button starts **disabled** until the box is typed. On draft VLX-000007 the menu is only Email invoice / Preview PDF / Download PDF / Void invoice — no public-link entries, since a draft has no token yet. Note: the confirm is case-INSENSITIVE — lowercase `rotate` also releases the button, though the dialog shows `ROTATE`.)*

### Public render (open in incognito)
- [x] Loads without login. Header: tenant logo + company_name + support_url. Optional 3px accent bar. *(walked 2026-07-27.)*
- [x] Invoice meta: number (mono), amount due (large tabular), due date. *(walked 2026-07-27.)*
- [x] Bill-to + From columns. Line-items table with tabular numerals. *(walked 2026-07-28 on the same page: Bill-to and From columns render, line items in a table with tabular numerals, totals block beneath.)*
- [x] Totals: subtotal, optional discount, optional tax with rate, reverse-charge **or tax-exempt** notice if applicable (`Tax-exempt — <reason>` for an exempt customer). *(walked 2026-07-28 on two hosted pages. Taxed: VLX-000025 reads Subtotal $100.00 / **Sales Tax(7.25%)** $7.25 / Total $107.25 / Amount due $107.25 — the rate is rendered with the tax line, and no Discount row appears when there is no discount. Exempt: set D2 Void Session to `tax_status=exempt` with reason "501(c)(3) non-profit — certificate on file" through the billing-profile dialog (the reason field appears only once Exempt is chosen, and is required), then issued VLX-000026 for $40.00 — tax computes to 0, the reason is stamped on the invoice row, and the hosted page replaces the tax line with **`Tax-exempt — 501(c)(3) non-profit — certificate on file`** verbatim.)*
- [x] **Pay {amount}** primary button (brand_color). **Download PDF** secondary. *(walked 2026-07-27.)*
- [x] Footer: "Secured by Stripe" + "Powered by Velox Billing". *(walked 2026-07-27.)*

### Pay
- [x] Click Pay → `POST /v1/public/invoices/{token}/checkout` → Stripe Checkout. Pay with `4242…` → redirect to `{baseURL}/invoice/{token}?paid=1`. *(walked 2026-07-27, NIM-000248.)*
- [x] Provisional "Processing your payment…" banner (green spinner). *(walked 2026-07-28 — see the settle-poll box above.)*
- [x] Webhook arrives → invoice paid → page auto-refetches → "Paid on {date}" banner; Pay button gone. *(walked 2026-07-27: flipped to "Paid · Received on Oct 5, 2026" — sim-date for the pinned customer — within seconds; Pay gone; real PI on the invoice.)*
- [x] PDF still downloads on paid. *(walked 2026-07-27 — 200, 35KB.)*
- [x] **Card saved to customer (ADR-021)**: after a successful Pay, dashboard customer detail shows the card under "Payment method" with brand + last 4 + status "Active". *(walked 2026-07-27: hosted 4242 pay on a no-PM customer → "payment method setup completed" + payment_methods row visa/4242; the Stripe customer was lazily minted.)* The next invoice for this customer auto-charges this PM (no email + Update Payment round-trip). Holds for a customer who had no PM on file before — the Stripe customer is lazily minted on first Pay.
- [x] **3-D Secure challenge (`4000 0027 6000 3184`)**: Pay → the bank challenge dialog appears; while it is open the invoice stays `finalized`/`pending` (no premature settle). **Fail** the challenge → checkout shows "unable to authenticate your payment method", `payment_intent.payment_failed` delivers, invoice flips `payment_status=failed`, no money moves. Retry on the same session and **Complete** → `requires_action` → `succeeded` + `checkout.session.completed` webhooks, invoice paid, and the `?paid=1` return shows the green Paid banner in the same session. *(walked 2026-07-28: NIM-000264 $20.90 — fail leg then complete leg on one checkout session, states verified in DB + stripe listen at each step.)*
- [x] **Interactive decline suppresses email (ADR-023)**: Pay with `4000 0000 0000 0002` (decline) → invoice goes to `payment_status=failed` → activity timeline shows the lifecycle row "Payment failed" but NO "Payment-failed email sent" row (customer was watching). Mailpit shows zero new emails. Auto-charge decline (e.g. dunning retry) still emails — only the interactive flow suppresses. *(walked 2026-07-28 on NIM-000266: `payment_intent.payment_failed` delivered, `payment_status=failed`, Mailpit count unchanged. Timeline: the failure renders as "Payment recovery started" with the decline cause subline (dunning-start-cause fix + uniform-title revision, 2026-07-28) and the PI folded on; no email row appears, which is the ADR-023 assertion.)*

### Variants
- [x] Voided invoice → "Voided on {date}" banner, no Pay, PDF works. *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000018: banner "Voided on Aug 20, 2026 — this invoice is no longer owed.", zero buttons (no Pay), and the PDF still serves through the public token — HTTP 200, application/pdf, 34.5 KB. FOUND + FIXED while walking: the totals block still labelled the surviving figure "Amount due" on a voided (and uncollectible) invoice — amount_due_cents survives those transitions by design, so the page told a customer they owed money we had stopped collecting. Now reads "Invoice amount", matching the same-day fix on the payment-update page.)*
- [x] Draft invoice URL → 404. *(walked 2026-07-28: a well-formed but unminted token on the public route returns HTTP 404 — no distinction leaked between "draft" and "never existed".)*
- [x] Rotated → old URL 404, new works. *(walked 2026-07-26 via rotate-public-token: old 404, new 200.)*

### Security
- [x] Public JSON has no `tenant_id, subscription_id, tax_id, stripe_*_id`. *(walked 2026-07-26 — clean at both envelope and invoice levels; payload carries livemode:false for the test-mode banner.)*
- [x] 61+ req/min same IP → 429 with `Retry-After`. *(walked 2026-07-26 with Redis up: 60 pass, then 429 + `Retry-After: 60`. The earlier "fails open, no warning" note was wrong on both counts: with Redis down the boot WARN "redis not reachable — general/hosted rate limiters FAIL CLOSED in production…" fires, dev fail-open is the deliberate OWASP-anchored split, and production fails CLOSED — router.go documents the design.)*
- [x] Operator `POST /v1/invoices/{id}/rotate-public-token` requires `PermInvoiceWrite`. *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000018: a **publishable** key (the type that holds no tenant-wide scopes) gets 403 `forbidden` — "insufficient permissions: this key type does not have invoice:write access" — while the operator session rotates the token to a fresh vlx_pinv_. NOTE for future walks: Velox permissions are TYPE-derived, not per-key — an `api-keys` create body carrying a `permissions` array is silently ignored, and the type field is `key_type` (a `type` key silently yields a full secret key). Testing an authorization gate therefore requires choosing the right key TYPE.)*

## FLOW I11: `create_preview`

- [x] `POST /v1/invoices/create_preview {subscription_id}` → invoice shape with `id=null`, no DB row. *(walked 2026-07-26 — lines/totals shape, zero invoice rows created; requires customer_id too.)*
- [x] No `audit_log` row from a preview: open a customer detail page (the upcoming-invoice card fires `create_preview` on load), then open `/audit-log` → **no** new "Created invoice" row. *(walked 2026-07-26 — audit_log count unchanged across preview calls.)*
- [x] Plan-change confirmation dialog states the proration RULE before commit — it does **not** render a costed preview. *(rewritten 2026-07-29: the old wording claimed a preview and was never implemented. Walked on a live sub — selecting a new plan and toggling "Apply immediately" fires **no** preview request at all (network panel), and the dialog shows prose: "The remaining time on the current billing period will be prorated. A credit or charge is applied based on the price difference between plans." `create_preview` cannot answer this today either — its request takes only customer/subscription/period, so it prices the CURRENT plan and has no way to cost a prospective swap. Numeric swap preview is deferred; see the velox-ops register entry. The dialog does earn its keep on one thing: a plan whose billing timing differs is **excluded from the list**, with the reason stated inline — "1 plan with a different billing timing is not shown — a plan swap keeps its billing timing. To move to advance billing, cancel and create a new subscription." — matching the ADR-031 cross-cadence block.)*
- [x] Cost-dashboard projection populated when engine returns a value. *(walked 2026-07-26 — the customer page's upcoming-invoice card rendered base $29.00 + Usage $8.00 from the preview.)*
- [x] **`in_advance` preview** (ADR-031): for a sub on an `in_advance` plan, preview's `billing_period_start/end` is the **upcoming** period, and the base line carries the "in advance for upcoming period" suffix. Same structure as the in_arrears preview — only the period selection and that suffix differ. *(walked 2026-07-29 against `POST /v1/invoices/create_preview` with a purpose-built pair. in_advance (I11 Advance Sub on a new $40 in_advance plan, current period **Jul 29 → Aug 1**): preview returns **Aug 1 → Sep 1** — the upcoming period, not the current one — with the line "D1 Advance Monthly - base fee (qty 1, **in advance for upcoming period**)" at 4000. in_arrears control (D1 CL rec, current period Sep 1 → Oct 1): preview returns **Sep 1 → Oct 1** — the CURRENT period — with "D1 Monthly - base fee (qty 1)" and no suffix. Both return one `base_fee` line and a single-currency `totals` array whose amount equals the plan's base price; the amounts differ only because the two plans are priced differently ($40 vs $31), so the totals were compared structurally, not numerically.)*

## FLOW I12: One-off invoice composer

> Walkthrough Co now carries **manual tax at 7.25% ("Sales Tax")** as its standing baseline, set during this walk. It was 0% before, which made every tax assertion in the tenant vacuous. Invoices stamp their own tax facts at finalize, so older fixtures are unaffected.

- [x] Customer detail → "New invoice" → composer shows Currency + Payment terms (Net 30 default) + line editor. *(walked 2026-07-28 on I6 Chrome Co: dialog carries Currency ($ USD), Payment terms defaulting to **Net 30** with the "Due 30 days after the invoice is issued." hint, an Add-line editor (Description / Type / Qty / Unit ($)), optional service period + memo, and a running totals block.)*
- [x] Enter three lines at `3333` / `3333` / `3334` cents → Subtotal ticks to $100.00; Tax row reads "Calculated at finalize". *(walked 2026-07-28: per-line echoes read "= $33.33 / $33.33 / $33.34", Subtotal **$100.00**, Tax row verbatim **"Calculated at finalize"**, Total (before tax) $100.00.)*
- [x] Save draft → exactly ONE `POST /v1/invoices` (no follow-up `add-line-item` calls); row appears with `status=draft`, `subscription_id=null`, `billing_reason=manual`, `tax_amount_cents=0`. *(walked 2026-07-28 → VLX-000025: the network panel shows a single `POST /v1/invoices` → **201** with no `add-line-item` request after it, and the row is exactly draft / subscription_id NULL / billing_reason `manual` / tax 0 / subtotal 10000 / net terms 30.)*
- [x] Tenant Tax = manual 7.25%, Finalize → `tax_amount_cents=725`, `SUM(line tax)=725`, `total_amount_cents=10725`, `due_at = issued_at + 30d`. The residual is placed by **largest remainder** (ADR-046), NOT dumped on the positionally-last line — this box claimed the latter until 2026-07-28, which is the behaviour ADR-046 explicitly supersedes. *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000025 — the ADR's own canonical example: top-down total 725¢ (not the bottom-up 726¢), lines taxed **242 / 241 / 242** against 3333 / 3333 / 3334. Floors are 241/241/241 = 723; the two spare cents go to the largest remainders — the $33.34 line (.715) and then the first .6425 on a stable order tie-break. `due_at` is exactly issued_at + 30d.)*
- [x] **Manual invoice on a clock-pinned customer discloses simulation (ADR-030 addendum, ADR-099)** *(walked 2026-07-26: NIM-000257 — detail-page test-clock banner, list-row Simulated chip, issued at sim-time Sep 26, is_simulated=true.)* — pin a customer to a test clock (no subscription needed), Advance the clock, then create a one-off invoice from Customer Detail → New invoice. The invoice's detail page shows the amber test-clock **banner** and its Invoices-list row the **Simulated** chip, the issued/paid dates are simulated frozen-time, and `invoices.is_simulated = true` in DB.
- [x] **A customer's credit balance pays a manual invoice at finalize (ADR-088)** *(walked 2026-07-26 during I4: NIM-000251 fully covered → Paid, balance drained; NIM-000252/253 partial application → due reduced by exactly the balance.)* — grant a customer credit, compose a one-off invoice above the balance, Finalize. The invoice shows Credits Applied for the full balance and the card is charged exactly the remainder; with balance ≥ total the invoice lands **Paid** with no charge. The credit ledger shows the applied entry; balance is drained.
- [x] **One-off invoice due countdown reads simulated time on the Invoices list (2026-07-08)** *(walked 2026-07-26: NIM-000257 Net-7, clock advanced past due → list badge "Past due 2d" red, counted in sim-time not wall-clock.)* — finalize that one-off invoice with a short term (e.g. Net 7), then advance the clock past its due date. On the **Invoices list**, the row's **due badge** reads **"Past due"** (red), matching the simulation — NOT a green "Due in Nd" counted against wall-clock. (Before the fix, a one-off invoice — no subscription — fell through the sub-only clock lookup to wall-clock.) A one-off invoice on a **wall-clock** (non-pinned) customer still counts against real time.
- [x] **The composer's Terms are honored exactly** *(walked 2026-07-26: Net 7 → due = issued + 7d exact (NIM-000257); Due on receipt → 0 days, Due == Issued, header reads "Due on receipt" (NIM-000258).)* — on that one-off invoice the header **Terms** matches the term picked (e.g. Net 7 → "Net 7") and equals `Due − Issued`, **not** the tenant default; picking **Due on receipt** is honored as 0 days → `Due == Issued` and Terms reads "Due on receipt" (NOT silently coerced to Net 30).
- [x] **The Period field is absent unless a Service period is set** *(walked 2026-07-26: NIM-000257 has no period → no Period row; NIM-000258 with service period shows it.)* — a one-off invoice has no billing cycle by default (see the service-period item below).
- [x] **Manual invoice Issued/Due anchor to finalize, not draft-create** *(walked 2026-07-26: NIM-000258 composed Sep 26 sim, clock advanced, finalized Oct 5 → Issued Oct 5; Activity shows created Sep 26 vs finalized Oct 5 as distinct rows.)* — compose a draft on a clock-pinned customer, **Advance the clock**, then Finalize. The header **Issued** date is the *finalize* moment (the advanced clock time), NOT the earlier draft-create time, and **Due** = Issued + the term. On the activity timeline the **Invoice created** and **Invoice finalized** rows show *different* timestamps (created = compose time, finalized = the later finalize time), not the same instant. Cycle invoices are unaffected (born finalized at build time).
- [x] **Manual invoice with a service period** *(walked 2026-07-26: NIM-000258 — detail Period reads "Jun 1, 2026 – Jun 30, 2026" inclusive over the half-open wire; dialog enforces both-or-neither.)* — Customer Detail → **New invoice** → add a line → set **Service period** to e.g. `2026-06-01` to `2026-06-30` (inclusive) → create. The invoice **Period** reads **"Jun 1, 2026 – Jun 30, 2026"** (inclusive last day) on the detail page, the Invoices-list Period column, and the PDF. Leaving the Service period blank creates a one-off invoice with **no** Period row (unchanged). Entering only one of the two dates, or a start after the end, is rejected in the dialog (both-or-neither, start ≤ end).
- [x] **Manual invoice collection mirrors cycle invoices** *(walked 2026-07-26: no-card NIM-000256 → setup-payment email + auto_charge_pending=true, no invoice email; with-card NIM-000250 → synchronous auto-charge + "Payment receipt emailed" timeline row, no invoice email.)* — finalize a manual invoice for a customer with **no saved card**: no "your invoice" email is auto-sent; instead the customer receives a **"set up payment method"** email and `invoices.auto_charge_pending = true` (the scheduler charges automatically once a card is attached). For a customer **with a saved card**: the invoice auto-charges, the customer gets a **payment receipt** on success (no separate invoice email), and `auto_charge_pending` stays false. Either way the operator can still send the invoice explicitly via the **Send** button. Matches what a subscription-cycle invoice does at finalize.

## FLOW I13: Timeline completeness

- [x] Tax-deferred invoice that auto-finalizes via tax retry → `invoice.finalized` lands in the webhook event stream. *(walked 2026-07-28 on two invoices whose tax deferred at a real cycle close and resolved later: VLX-000024 (auto-finalized by the billing-profile flush) and VLX-000023 (operator Retry tax) each emitted exactly one `invoice.finalized` event carrying `status=finalized`, `payment_status=pending` and the resolved totals. The event fires from `Service.Finalize`, so the auto-finalize chain notifies integrators the same as an operator click.)*
- [x] Issue a credit note on a paid invoice → the invoice Activity shows a dated "Credit note issued / Refund issued — $X" row (CN number + reason as subtext). *(walked 2026-07-26: NIM-000250 → "Credit note issued · CN-000032 — other · $605.00".)*
- [x] Mark an invoice uncollectible (operator menu) → Activity shows "Marked uncollectible — written off as bad debt" with the timestamp; the page STOPS polling (network tab quiet). *(walked 2026-07-26 via the dunning write-off on NIM-000244 — row exact; polling-stop not re-checked this walk.)*
- [x] Record an offline payment → Activity row reads "Payment recorded (offline)", not a card payment. *(walked 2026-07-26: NIM-000244 — row + "Recorded by an operator — cheque, wire, or other out-of-band payment" subtext.)*
- [x] Plan-change with immediate proration → the subscription Activity row reads "Plan changed · <old> → <new> · Immediate · Proration invoice $X". *(walked 2026-07-29 on a purpose-built in_advance pair: I11 Advance Sub swapped D1 Advance Monthly → D1 Advance Pro immediately, and the row reads verbatim **"Plan changed | D1 Advance Monthly → D1 Advance Pro · Immediate · Proration invoice $3.11"** with the actor, matching the toast "Proration invoice created for $3.11". Two things the walk surfaced on the way. (1) The swap is BLOCKED while the current period's invoice is unpaid — 409 `unpaid_invoice_blocks_change`, "invoice VLX-000031 for the current period is unpaid (4.15 USD outstanding); settle or void it before changing this subscription" — and the dialog surfaces that verbatim in a toast with a Request ID and a Copy ID button. Recording an offline payment cleared it. (2) Only the IMMEDIATE variant is walked here; the "Scheduled plan change applied" boundary row and the "Spending threshold crossed" row named in this box are still unwalked — they need a clock ride to the boundary and a configured spend threshold respectively.)*
- [x] Scheduled-cancel sub: period bar reads Period Start → Period End → **Cancels** (no "Next Billing"); paused-collection sub shows **Collection resumes <date>**. *(walked 2026-07-29 with a control. Baseline running sub: Period Start → Period End → **Next Billing** Oct 1. Cancel → "At period end" on I6 Unc2 Sub → the bar becomes Period Start → Period End → **Cancels Sep 1, 2026** and "Next Billing" is gone. Pause → auto-resume Sep 15 on a second sub → the third point becomes **Collection resumes Sep 15, 2026**, with the banner reading "(auto-resumes Sep 15, 2026, 12:00 AM UTC)". A pause with NO resume date — what dunning's `pause` final action creates — reads **Collection paused / Indefinite** instead, so the box's "resumes <date>" wording only applies to a timed pause. The auto-resume date picker is clock-aware: on this clock-pinned sub every date before the clock's frozen Sep 2 is disabled, and the enabled range starts exactly at Sep 2.)*
- [x] **One lane, the entity's calendar (ADR-104; supersedes the two-lane split this box previously asserted)** — the invoice page renders a single Activity timeline. Every row's primary date is on the invoice's own calendar (simulated when clock-pinned; every fact is write-time anchored — outbox sim anchor for emails, ADR-102 dual stamps for attempts, entity-derived stamps for the rest). A row that also touched the real world shows a muted **"Recorded <wall>"** subline (Invariant A), and same-instant cascades order by the causal ladder — created → finalized → attempts → paid → credit notes → notification last. On a live invoice nothing changes: one calendar, no sublines, zero simulation vocabulary (Invariant B). *(re-walked 2026-07-29 post-build — see the ADR-104 walk annotations below for the live evidence; the pre-ADR-104 two-lane behavior this box previously recorded was walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000006/VLX-000012 and is retired, not disproven.)*
- [x] **ADR-104 walk: an anchored email joins the story** — on a clock-pinned invoice, trigger a fresh email (operator "Email invoice", or a receipt via settle) → the row renders in Activity at the invoice's **simulated** instant with a muted **"Recorded <wall>"** subline, positioned after the money event that caused it (rankEmail last). *(walked 2026-07-29 on VLX-000034, clock frozen at Sep 2 2026: More actions → Email invoice → Send. Outbox row `vlx_emob_d9kr7mb…` wrote `sim_effective_at=2026-09-02 12:00:00`, `test_clock_id=vlx_tclk_f0eeaab…`, `created_at=2026-07-29 08:03:37`. Timeline renders "Invoice emailed to customer · Sep 2, 2026, 12:00 PM UTC" **last** in the lane with subline "Recorded Jul 29, 2026, 8:03 AM UTC". The SAME invoice carries the counter-evidence: an identical email sent 5 minutes earlier — before the handler-binding fix — wrote a NULL anchor and still sorts at its real 7:58 AM time near the top with no subline. Both behaviours, one screen: `adr104-unified-timeline-walk.png`.)*
- [x] **ADR-104 walk: live invoices are untouched** — a wall-clock invoice's timeline shows one lane, emails at their real times, **no** Recorded sublines, and no simulation vocabulary anywhere on the page. *(walked 2026-07-29 on VLX-000031, a wall-clock paid invoice: one Activity card — created → finalized → recovery started → "Customer notified — set up payment method" → "Payment recorded (offline) $4.15" (copy since renamed to "Payment received (offline)") — all at real Jul 29 times, zero `Recorded <time>` sublines, no banner. NOTE: this page also uses "recorded" in its OTHER product sense (manual out-of-band payment entry); ADR-104 Invariant C records the collision and why the timeline word still wins.)*
- [x] **ADR-104 walk: teardown takes the emails** — delete a test clock → its customers' `email_outbox` rows (anchored by `test_clock_id`) are gone with the rest of the graph; the customer page shows no orphan sent-emails from the deleted simulation. CI-locked: the teardown completeness test now discovers `test_clock_id`-carrying tables. *(walked 2026-07-29 on a SCRATCH clock so no documented fixture was touched: created clock frozen at Mar 1 2027 + pinned customer + one-off invoice VLX-000036, finalized, emailed. Two anchored outbox rows resulted — the operator `invoice` email AND the finalize-time `payment_setup_request`, both stamped `sim_effective_at=2027-03-01 12:00`, which also proves the collectAtFinalize binding fix. DELETE the clock → `outbox_rows_left=0`, `invoices_left=0`, `customers_left=0`, while `walkthrough_clock_intact=1` and VLX-000034's 3 emails survived untouched.)*
- [x] **ADR-104 Invariant A covers credit notes + dunning events (0164)** — issue a credit note on a frozen-clock invoice → the timeline row carries "Recorded <real time>" beneath its simulated date, same grammar as emails/attempts; a pre-0164 row on the same invoice stays bare (no backfill). *(walked 2026-07-29 on VLX-000038, the operator-found gap fixed same day: CN-000009 "$1.50 — Recorded-line walk" renders "Jun 1, 2027 · Recorded Jul 29, 2026, 10:32 AM UTC" directly beside bare pre-migration CN-000008 — both behaviours one screen, `cn-recorded-line-walk.png`. CI: TestCreditNote_RecordedAt_DualStamp + TestDunningEvent_RecordedAt_DualStamp, both mutation-verified.)*
- [x] **Frozen-instant groups order by the real sequence (reality amendment)** — on a clock-parked invoice, perform actions in an order that contradicts the causal grammar (email → credit note → record payment): the timeline renders them in the ORDER YOU ACTED, payment last, exactly as a wall-clock invoice would — and an invoice whose instant-group contains any unstamped legacy row keeps the old canonical order (never a fabricated sequence). *(walked 2026-07-29 on fresh VLX-000039, E2E clock parked Jun 1 2027: created → finalized → emailed → notified → CN-000010 → "Payment received (offline)" LAST with Recorded 11:28 — the ladder would have put payment above both; `reality-order-walk.png`. Gate proven on VLX-000038 (bare CN-000008): API row order byte-identical to pre-change ladder. CI: TestSortInvoiceTimeline_RealityPass, four regimes.)*
- [x] **Manual invoice actions carry Recorded lines (audit enrichment)** — on a frozen-clock invoice, operator finalize / void / mark-uncollectible / record-offline-payment rows each show "Recorded <real time>" beneath their simulated date, joined read-time from the audit log (exact action keys; the update-flavored transitions match on the frozen `metadata.action` discriminator). A transition with no audit row stays bare. *(walked 2026-07-29 on VLX-000038: Record offline payment at 10:47 real → "Payment recorded (offline) · Jun 1, 2027 · Recorded Jul 29, 2026, 10:47 AM UTC"; created/finalized rows show their 8:48 AM stamps from the create/finalize audit rows — `lifecycle-recorded-walk.png`. Void/uncollectible mappings CI-locked by TestLifecycleRecordedStamps; card-settle paid-row lift rides the existing attempt fold. NOTE: the Invariant-C collision this walk exposed — three senses of "recorded" on one row — was RESOLVED same day by rename: the row now reads "Payment received (offline) · Entered by an operator …", leaving the timestamp label as the word's only sense (operator-ambiguity tiebreaker).)*
- [x] **ADR-104 vocabulary gate holds** — `internal/arch` fails CI on "wall-clock" / "Real-time activity" / the negation caption in operator-facing web-v2 source. Its first run caught live copy on five surfaces (both test-clock banner variants, two Simulated-badge tooltips, the audit-log dual-stamp tooltip, the customer clock picker) — all reworded to the contract's vocabulary.
- [~] **Void of a clock-pinned invoice with a pending PI shows no duplicate "Payment canceled" row** — finalize a clock-pinned invoice (pending PI), then Void it. The timeline shows the void but NOT a separate "Payment canceled" row. *(structural since ADR-103, 2026-07-28: the timeline no longer renders Stripe webhook rows at all, and `payment_intent.canceled` has no charge-attempt equivalent — a canceled PI is an attempt that never completed, and `pending` attempts do not render. There is nothing left that could emit the duplicate row this box was written to catch, so the old time-window fold that produced it is gone too. Not hand-walked: it needs an abandoned Checkout session on a clock-pinned invoice, and the assertion is now about code that no longer exists.)*
- [x] **Credit notes sit on the invoice's calendar whoever issued them (ADR-030 fix + ADR-104; supersedes the lane-split behavior this box previously recorded)** — an engine clawback and an operator-issued credit note on the same clock-pinned invoice both render in the single Activity lane at the invoice's simulated instants, ordered by the causal ladder. A credit note is a financial document — story-only, one calendar, no Recorded subline (its real-world issue moment lives in the audit log, which stays wall-clock). *(re-walked 2026-07-29 on VLX-000034 post-build: CN-000004 (engine clawback) and CN-000007 (operator, post-fix) both at Sep 2, 2026 in Activity. The same invoice also demonstrates the accepted legacy cost: CN-000005 and CN-000006 — created before the ADR-030 fix completed — carry pre-fix stamps/flags and render at their recorded positions; documented in ADR-104's Consequences, pre-launch fixtures only.)*

- [x] **A dunning resolution never renders beside its own cause row (ADR-020 fold, fixed 2026-07-26):** resolve a run as write-off → the timeline shows "Marked uncollectible — written off as bad debt" with NO bare "Dunning resolved" twin at the same instant; a void-flavored resolution folds into "Invoice voided" the same way (payment_recovered already folded into "Invoice paid"). The fold is gated on the invoice's own transition field, so a run resolved whose invoice never transitioned (failed propagation) KEEPS the dunning row as the only surviving record. *(found live on NIM-000244/NIM-000233; automated + mutation-verified: `TestPaymentTimeline_ResolvedRowFoldsIntoCauseRow`.)*

## Dunning

## FLOW D1: Retry cycle + escalation

- [x] Decline card → run billing → dunning run created. Page shows stat cards (Active, Escalated, Recovered, At Risk $) + tab filters with counts. *(walked 2026-07-28 on the fresh Walkthrough Co tenant: 0341 card, cycle close declined VLX-000001 → run created with write-time cause payment_failed; page read Active 1 / Escalated 0 / Recovered 0 / At risk $4.00 — every number explainable — and later Escalated 2 / Recovered 1 after the escalation + card-less recovery legs. Tabs All/Active/Escalated/Recovered present.)*
- [x] Sidebar Dunning badge shows count. *(walked 2026-07-28: badge read "Dunning 1" for the ONE wall-clock run (VLX-000004) while three simulated runs existed — the badge/analytics counts deliberately exclude test-clock rows (ADR-086 sim gating), so a clock-only tenant shows no badge.)*
- [x] Run state Active, "No retries yet", `next_action_at` scheduled. *(walked 2026-07-28: VLX-000001's run row — active, "No retries yet", Next Retry "in 2d" (= failure + 3d grace, sim-relative), Started "12h ago" sim-relative.)*
- [x] Backdate `next_action_at` → next tick increments attempt count. *(walked 2026-07-28 via clock advance past the due instant: attempt 0→1, later 1→2→3 in ONE advance. This walk found + fixed the charge-lease starvation: a definitive decline held the per-invoice lease its full 5-minute window, so back-to-back due retries in one catchup were skipped as transient and the loop exited "no progress" — the chokepoint now releases the lease on definitive outcomes; ambiguous outcomes still hold it.)*
- [x] After max retries → state Escalated. *(walked 2026-07-28: after attempt 3 the run escalated `retries_exhausted` (final_action pause); dunning page shows the escalated pill + "3 retries".)*
- [x] **Card-less invoice enters dunning and reaches a terminal (ADR-060)**: a finalized `auto_charge_pending` invoice for a customer with **no saved card** gets a dunning run created on the next scheduler tick (no charge attempted) and escalates through grace + retries to the policy `final_action` (e.g. `pause`) — instead of being retried forever with no terminal. Adding a card mid-campaign → the auto-charge sweep collects it and the run resolves `payment_recovered`. With dunning disabled for the tenant, the invoice stays un-dunned (deliberate — same as the declined-card path). *(walked 2026-07-28, two card-less customers on one clock: VLX-000002 enrolled `no_payment_method` at the close tick, rode 3 reminder ticks → escalated `retries_exhausted` (pause); VLX-000003 enrolled the same tick, 4242 attached mid-campaign → the sweep collected it on the next advance → payment succeeded, run resolved `payment_recovered` at attempt 0. Dunning-disabled clause walked earlier on Nimbus NIM-000269/270.)*

## FLOW D2: Resolution

- [x] "Payment recovered" → invoice marked **paid** (`paid_at` stamped; clock-pinned invoices land in sim-time), run closed — via the SAME offline-payment writer as the invoice page's Record-offline-payment dialog: `stripe_payment_intent_id` gets the `out_of_band:` prefix, the in-flight-charge guard applies, and `invoice.payment_recorded` + `invoice.paid` webhooks both fire. *(walked 2026-07-26 on NIM-000241 (3-retry escalated run): run resolved + invoice paid/succeeded, timeline folds to one "Invoice paid" row with `attempt_count: 3` ("after 3 retry attempts"); pre-fix a raw MarkPaid("") left no out_of_band marker and skipped the payment_recorded webhook. Automated: `TestResolveRun_PaymentRecovered_RoutesThroughOfflinePaymentWriter`.)*
- [x] **Unknown resolution values are 422s, not stored verbatim** — POST `/v1/dunning/runs/{id}/resolve` with any value outside `payment_recovered` / `manually_resolved` / `invoice_not_collectible` (typos, engine-only states like `retries_exhausted`, free text) → 422 naming the allowed values; the run is untouched. A CHECK constraint (m0158) backstops every writer. *(found live 2026-07-26: a typo'd `payment_received` was stored verbatim, silently skipped mark-paid, and rendered as a raw slug in the dashboard. Automated: `TestResolveRun_RejectsUnknownResolution`.)*
- [x] "Void invoice" (resolution `manually_resolved`) → invoice **voided** (`status='voided'`); any applied credits **reversed** to the customer's balance; collection **stopped at Stripe** — live Checkout sessions expired, and the PaymentIntent canceled when Stripe allows it. *(walked 2026-07-28 on VLX-000004: run resolved `manually_resolved`, invoice voided. THIS WALK FOUND A GAP: Stripe REFUSES `cancel` on PaymentIntents it created for Checkout ("Try expiring the Checkout Session instead"), so a hosted-page attempt's session stayed `open` at Stripe after the void — a voided invoice still payable until its ≤1h ExpiresAt. Fixed via a `StopCollection` seam (expire sessions first, then cancel the PI where cancelable) shared by the operator void and the dunning void-resolve. Re-walked on VLX-000005 side by side: pre-fix session `open`, post-fix session `expired` at Stripe. Credit-reversal clause covered by the atomic reversal inside `Void` — CI-locked, no credits on this fixture.)*
- [x] The void option reads **"Void invoice"** with destructive (red) styling and copy steering offline payments to "Payment recovered" — NOT a benign "Manually resolved / offline payment" label (which would trap an operator into voiding an invoice they actually collected). *(walked 2026-07-28: dialog options verbatim — "Payment recovered / Customer has paid…", "Void invoice / Annul the invoice and stop collection — e.g. billed in error or waived. Reverses any applied credits and cancels the pending charge… For a payment collected offline, use \"Payment recovered\" instead.", "Write off invoice / Marks the invoice as uncollectible (bad debt)…".)*

## FLOW D4: Self-service payment update

- [x] Trigger payment failure → email/log carries `http://localhost:5173/update-payment?token=vlx_pt_…`. *(walked 2026-07-28 on the fresh tenant: finalizing VLX-000006 for a no-PM customer sent "Set up payment for invoice VLX-000006"; the CTA href carried the vlx_pt_ token URL.)*
- [x] Open in incognito → page loads without login, shows customer + invoice + amount, "Secured by Stripe". *(walked 2026-07-28: no session, page rendered "Secure payment update" with customer, VLX-000006, Amount Due $42.00, and the Stripe assurance line. THIS WALK FOUND A LIE: the page never read invoice status, so a VOIDED invoice (VLX-000005) still rendered "Payment method needed — Add a payment method to pay this invoice" with "Amount Due $25.00". Fixed: the validate endpoint returns invoice_status and the page states the truth for terminal invoices — "This invoice was canceled / No payment is needed. You can still add a payment method so future invoices are collected automatically." — with the figure relabeled "Invoice amount". Owing invoices are unchanged.)*
- [x] Click Update → Stripe Checkout setup → new card → redirect → the `setup_intent.succeeded` webhook records the card. The **customer detail page now shows it** under "Payment method" (brand + last 4), and the previously-unpaid invoice auto-charges on the next sweep (or clock advance for a clock-pinned customer). The card persists because the Checkout session now stamps `velox_customer_id` on `setup_intent_data.metadata`, so the SetupIntent is self-resolving (the resolver-by-`customer` fallback remains as a backstop). *(walked 2026-07-28 end-to-end: 4242 through Checkout → setup_intent.succeeded recorded visa ••4242 → the auto-charge sweep collected VLX-000006 on its next tick → invoice paid, amount_paid 4200.)*
- [x] **A saved card is never dropped on webhook ordering**: if `setup_intent.succeeded` is processed before the customer↔Stripe-id link is written (e.g. it arrives before `checkout.session.completed`), the handler returns a transient 5xx (no dedup row written) and Stripe **redelivers** — the card lands on the redelivery once the link resolves. Pre-fix this returned 200, was dedup-marked, and the card was permanently dropped (PM never shown, invoice stuck `auto_charge_pending`). A setup-intent with no payment method is acked (nothing to attach). *(CI-locked rather than hand-walked: forcing the ordering needs an artificial mid-write state, and `TestHandleWebhook_SetupIntent_UnresolvedCustomerRedelivers` (internal/payment) pins all three properties exactly — transient error so Stripe redelivers, attacher NOT called, and NO dedup row written (a dedup row would short-circuit the redelivery and drop the card permanently). Verified green 2026-07-28.)*
- [x] First-time customer (no Stripe Customer yet — the usual no-saved-card case this email targets): clicking Update creates the Stripe Customer on demand and opens Checkout, NOT a "customer does not have a Stripe payment setup" error. *(walked 2026-07-28: D4 Self Serve had no Stripe customer before the click; Checkout opened and the customer row carries a stripe_customer_id afterwards.)*
- [x] A failed attempt does NOT spend the link: if the Update click errors before Checkout opens (e.g. Stripe momentarily unreachable), re-opening the same link still works — only a successful Checkout open consumes the token. *(walked 2026-07-28 on a purpose-built Stripe-less tenant — the case the code names: POST checkout returned 502 with the ADR-026 Tier-2 neutral copy ("We couldn't start the payment update right now…", no config detail leaked), and re-validating the SAME token still returned the invoice — the link was not spent. Ordering holds because EnsureStripeCustomer runs before the consume CAS.)*
- [x] Re-open same URL after a successful use → "Link expired or invalid". Random token → same. No token → "No payment update token provided". *(walked 2026-07-28: after the successful Checkout the same token returned 401 invalid_token "invalid or expired token"; a random vlx_pt_ value returned the identical shape — no oracle distinguishing consumed from never-existed.)*

---

## FLOW D5: Dunning policy admin (CRUD + assignment + terminal actions)

Policy configuration surface — distinct from the dunning state machine under catchup (FLOW TC5).

- [x] **Policy CRUD invariants** — `/dunning-policies` admin page: create a new policy with `max_retry_attempts=5` and `retry_schedule=[72h, 120h]` → save rejects with inline error naming the missing entries. Delete the default policy → rejected ("promote another policy first"). Delete a non-default policy with assigned customers → rejected ("N customer(s) still assigned; reassign first"). *(walked 2026-07-28 on the fresh tenant: the create dialog refused 5-retries-with-2-entries inline — "Need 4 entries for 5 retries." + "Missing 2 entries." — and saved once corrected, born `is_default=true` as the tenant's first policy. The DEFAULT row deliberately offers NO delete affordance in the UI (only Edit; the non-default row carries Make-default / Edit / Delete), and the API rejects it anyway — 422 "cannot delete the default dunning policy — promote another policy first". After assigning a customer to WT Enterprise (card shows "1 assigned"), deleting it → 422 "cannot delete policy — 1 customer(s) still assigned; reassign them first".)*
- [x] **Four terminal actions (ADR-036 amendment)** — dropdown options: `Pause collection (keep drafting invoices)`, `Cancel subscription`, `Mark invoice uncollectible`, `Leave open — manual review`. *(walked 2026-07-28 on the fresh tenant's New-policy dialog: all four present verbatim, in that order.)*

## Credits & Credit Notes

## FLOW C1: Credits lifecycle

- [x] Grant $50 expires 30d → balance $50, ledger Expires column populated. *(walked 2026-07-29 through the real Grant Credits dialog on a fresh clock-pinned customer (C1 Credits A, clock parked Sep 1 2027): $50 with expiry picked as Sep 30 2027 → ledger row `grant | 5000 | expires 2027-09-30 | "C1 walk - 30d expiring grant"` with **created_at = 2027-09-01 09:00** — the grant is stamped on the customer's calendar, not wall-clock. The dialog also gates the write behind a typed confirm ("Grant $50.00 to C1 Credits A (carded)?") and carries one idempotency key per dialog open.)*
- [x] **Credit-expiry date reads the customer's simulated now (ADR-086):** on a clock-pinned customer advanced to e.g. 2027, the Grant Credit dialog's expiry-date picker floor and its "must be a future date" check reject a real-today date (it's *past* in simulated time) — anchored on the clock's `frozen_time`, not wall-clock. Same on the invoice composer's prepaid-credit line. *(**THIS BOX FOUND A BUG, fixed same session.** Walked 2026-07-29 on a customer pinned to a Sep-1-2027 clock: the picker opened on **July 2026** with the floor at REAL today — offering ~13 months of sim-PAST dates. Root cause: the dialog resolved the selected customer from the page's `customers` list, which is fetched scoped to customers who ALREADY have a balance (`ids=<balanceCustomerIds>`), so on a **first-ever grant** the lookup missed, `test_clock_id` was undefined, and `useEffectiveNow` fell back to wall-clock — i.e. the guard was absent in the most common case. The backend is NOT fooled (`clock.Now(ctx)` → 422 "must be in the future — a grant that expires at or before now is dead on arrival", verified by probing a Dec-2026 expiry), so no born-expired credit can be created; the harm is operator confusion — a date that IS future on the calendar you were shown, rejected as past. Fixed by falling back to the combobox's own by-id fetch under its EXACT query key (cache-shared, no extra request). Re-walked: August 2027 fully disabled, September 1 onward enabled — the floor is now exactly `frozen_time`.)*
- [x] Run billing → applied, amount_due reduced, Stripe charged remainder. Ledger entry "Applied to invoice <number>". *(walked 2026-07-29 on C1 Credits A (saved 4242 via real Checkout, $50 credit): an $84.73 invoice (VLX-000046, $79 + 7.25% tax) drew the full $50 credit and charged the **remainder $34.73** to the card — `pi_3Tyccj…`, invoice paid, and 5000 + 3473 = 8473 reconciles to the cent. Ledger row reads verbatim `usage | -5000 | "Applied to invoice VLX-000046"` linked to the invoice id.)*
- [x] Grant $500 + $79 invoice → fully credited, amount_due $0, balance $421, Stripe NOT charged. *(walked 2026-07-29 in its STRONGEST form — on the customer WITH a saved card, so "Stripe NOT charged" is a real assertion rather than a vacuous one: $500 grant + VLX-000047 ($84.73 with tax) → `credits_applied 8473`, `amount_due 0`, `amount_paid 0`, **`stripe_payment_intent_id` absent**, status paid. Balance $415.27 (the box's $421 assumes a tax-free $79; ours carries 7.25% tax) and the ledger sums exactly: 5000 + 50000 − 5000 − 8473 = 41527.)*
- [x] Deduct $20 → confirmation → balance reduced, ledger entry. *(walked 2026-07-29 through the Credits page: Deduct → typed amount + description → confirm dialog "Deduct $20.00 from C1 Credits A (carded)?" → balance $415.27 → **$395.27**, ledger row typed **Adjustment** "C1 walk - deduction probe" −$20.00 with the running-balance column showing $395.27. Ledger readability observed alongside (covers part of C2b): every row dated **Sep 1, 2027** — the customer's clock, not wall-clock — with a Simulated badge, type filters (Grant/Deduction/Adjustment), a CSV export, and the expiring grant showing "Expires Sep 30, 2027" beneath its description.)*
- [x] **Credits always precede card charges**: grant a customer a credit balance smaller than an unpaid flagged invoice → next scheduler tick (or clock Advance) charges only the remainder, never the full amount; balance fully covering the invoice → invoice flips to paid with NO Stripe charge and the flag clears. Same on a dunning retry: credits granted after the failure reduce (or fully settle) the retried charge. *(walked 2026-07-29 across BOTH collection paths. **Finalize path** (carded customer, VLX-000046 $84.73): the $50 balance drew first and Stripe was asked for exactly the **$34.73 remainder** — never the gross. **Sweep path** (card-less customer, VLX-000048 $107.25, `auto_charge_pending=true`) in two clock advances: a partial $40 grant → advance → `credits_applied 4000`, **due $67.25**, still finalized + still flagged, no PI — credits drew down without any full-amount attempt; then an $80 grant → advance → `credits_applied 10725`, **due $0, status paid, amount_paid 0, stripe_payment_intent_id absent**, and the `auto_charge_pending` flag cleared. Full coverage on a customer WITH a saved card is proven separately on VLX-000047 (no PI created). The dunning-retry clause is the same code path (credits are applied before the charge attempt in the shared collection chokepoint) but was NOT separately walked — it needs a declined-card fixture mid-campaign; flagged here rather than claimed.)*

## FLOW C2: Credit notes

- [x] **In-flight payment blocks an amount-reducing credit note**: `psql -c "UPDATE invoices SET payment_status='processing' WHERE id='<a finalized, unpaid invoice>'"` → POST a credit note for it → **409** "cannot credit-note an invoice whose payment is in flight — settle or cancel the payment first". Reset to `pending` → the same credit note succeeds. (Operator gate; the automated clawback defers instead — see the Part-B flow below.) *(walked 2026-07-29 on VLX-000049: while `processing` → 409 verbatim "cannot credit-note an invoice whose payment is in flight — settle or cancel the payment first, or wait for charge reconciliation"; reset to `pending` → the same CN created and issued as CN-000018.)*
- [x] **In-flight payment blocks Void and Mark-uncollectible (ADR-059)**: with a finalized invoice forced to `payment_status='processing'` (psql, as above) → POST `/v1/invoices/{id}/void` → **409** "a charge is in flight … wait for it to settle or cancel it before voiding"; POST `/v1/invoices/{id}/mark-uncollectible` → **409** "… before marking uncollectible". Reset to `failed` (or `pending`) → both succeed. (`RecordOfflinePayment` is blocked the same way. `unknown` behaves like `processing` EXCEPT when the invoice is **parked** — `unknown` with no PaymentIntent id — where mark-uncollectible is deliberately allowed as the only exit; see FLOW I4c.) *(walked 2026-07-29, all four writers blocked with DISTINCT per-action copy on VLX-000049 while `processing`: void "…before voiding", mark-uncollectible "…before marking uncollectible", record-payment "…before recording an offline payment", credit-note (above). Positive halves both walked: reset to `pending` → mark-uncollectible returned 200 → status `uncollectible`; a second fixture VLX-000050 forced `processing` → void 409, reset to `failed` → void 200 → status `voided`.)*
- [x] **A clawback on an in-flight source defers (ADR-059, Part B)** — on a clock-pinned in_advance sub whose paid prebill is forced to `payment_status='processing'` (psql), downgrade mid-cycle → the clawback credit note is created **`status='draft'`, `issue_pending=true`** and the prebill's `amount_due` is **unchanged**; the reconciler scan skips it while the source is in flight. *(**THIS BOX FOUND A MONEY BUG, fixed same session.** Walked 2026-07-30 with a control/treatment pair — two identical downgrades on identical paid in_advance prebills, differing ONLY in the source's payment_status. Control (`succeeded`): CN issued, customer balance **+$32.18**. Treatment (`processing`): **no credit-note row at all**, balance **$0** — and the item change had committed, so the customer kept the cheaper plan and the $32.18 they were owed existed nowhere. Root cause: the proration router split two ways (`!= PaymentSucceeded`) where reality is three, so in-flight sources were sent down the UNPAID amount_due-reduction branch, which on an already-paid source computes `min(grossCredit, amount_due) == 0` and returned nil **silently** — no row, no error, no log. `domain.IsInFlight`'s own doc says such a mutation must "refuse or defer", and DEFER was already built end-to-end (`creditnote.Issue` is the single chokepoint and leaves the note draft+issue_pending while the source is in flight); the router simply never let a draft exist. Fixed by routing in-flight to the draft path, capping in-flight funding invoices like PAID ones, and making the residual unreachable-case drop log ERROR instead of returning nil. Post-fix on the same fixture: **CN-000023 `draft` / `issue_pending=t` / $32.18 with `amount_due` unchanged**, and both handoff log lines present ("source payment in flight — clawback will be drafted", then "deferring clawback issue until source payment settles"). CI: `TestUpdateItem_Downgrade_InFlightSource_DraftsInsteadOfDroppingCredit` runs the same control/treatment pair over BOTH in-flight statuses, mutation-verified.)*
- [x] **…then settles down the correct branch once the source resolves** — set the source `payment_status='succeeded'` + one scheduler tick (or trigger the clawback reconciler): the draft flips to **`issued`** and the unused share lands as a **customer balance credit** (paid branch). Source = `failed` instead → it issues down the **reduce** branch (`amount_due` drops to the consumed portion). Source **voided or marked uncollectible** before settle → the draft is **voided** (orphan guard), no second tax reversal — including when the source was parked and never settled at all, which is the write-off's job to release (FLOW I4c). *(PAID branch walked 2026-07-30 on CN-000023: source set to `succeeded`, then **one clock advance** → `issued`, `issue_pending=f`, customer balance **+$32.18** — exactly what the settled-source control received. WALK NOTE worth keeping: the wall-clock reconciler scan carries `AND is_simulated = false` (ADR-086 sim-gating), so a clock-pinned draft is deliberately NOT issued by a scheduler tick — it needs a clock **advance** (the catchup path). Three ticks passed with the draft correctly untouched before the advance settled it. The `failed`→reduce branch and the voided-orphan branch were NOT walked here — they need their own fixtures; the branch selection itself lives in `creditnote.Issue` and is covered by the ADR-061 store tests.)*
- [x] Unpaid invoice → Issue credit "Billing error" $20 → no allocation inputs shown → Issue → amount_due reduced. *(walked 2026-07-29 on VLX-000049: $20 CN reason "Billing error" → issued as CN-000018 with **all three allocation channels at zero** (`refund 0 / credit 0 / out_of_band 0` — an unpaid invoice's CN has nothing to allocate, it just reduces the debt) and `amount_due` $53.62 → **$33.62**, exactly −$20.00.)*
- [x] Paid invoice ($100, fully card-paid) → enter $40 → defaults to Credit balance $40, Refund 0, Out-of-band 0 → Issue → customer balance +$40. *(walked 2026-07-30 in the real dialog on VLX-000052 ($100 + 7.25% = $107.25, fully card-paid via `pi_3TynHk…`, zero credits): typing Amount **$40** auto-defaulted **Credit balance 40** with Refund and Out-of-band left empty, and the reconciliation line read `Allocated $40.00 / $40.00 ✓` with Issue enabled. The $40-credit ISSUE itself was not fired — the same dialog was reused for the refund box below; the credit-to-balance grant path is proven separately by the atomicity box and by CN-000004/CN-000016 in earlier flows.)* DESIGN NOTE (checked, passes): the empty dialog reads `Allocated $0.00 / $0.00` which *looks* satisfied, but Issue is **disabled** in that state — a mis-click cannot issue a $0 credit note.
- [x] Same invoice → enter $30 + type $30 in Refund to card → Credit auto-fills to $0; Allocated $30 / $30 ✓ → Issue → Stripe refund processed; CN row label "refund"; refund_status=succeeded. *(walked 2026-07-30 on VLX-000052: typing $30 into Refund auto-filled Credit to **$0.00** and the line read `Allocated $30.00 / $30.00 ✓`; Issue produced **CN-000019** `issued`, `refund 3000 / credit 0 / oob 0`, `refund_status=succeeded`, `stripe_refund_id=re_3TynHk…`. **Provider-side verified** with `stripe refunds retrieve`: the refund exists at Stripe with `status: succeeded`, `amount: 3000 USD`, against the same PaymentIntent Velox charged — real money out and back, not just a local row.)*
- [x] Mixed-paid invoice ($82.60 = $62.60 card + $20 credits) → enter $80 → type $62.60 in Refund to card → Credit auto-fills to $17.40 → Issue → Stripe refund $62.60 + credit grant $17.40; CN row label "refund + credit"; balance increases by $17.40. *(walked 2026-07-30 on a fixture built to the box's EXACT numbers — VLX-000053 total **$82.60 = $20.00 credits + $62.60 card**. The refund field's cap correctly reads **`max $62.60`** (the CARD portion, not the $82.60 total — the distinction that makes this case worth having); typing $62.60 auto-filled Credit to exactly **$17.40** and the line read `Allocated $80.00 / $80.00 ✓`. Issue produced **CN-000020** `issued`, `total 8000 = refund 6260 + credit 1740 + oob 0`, `refund_status=succeeded`, `stripe_refund_id=re_3TynLT…`, AND the ledger grant `+1740 "Credit note CN-000020 — Partial service credit"` carrying its source-CN link — balance went to exactly $17.40. Both channels of one CN settled through their own rails in one action.)*
- [x] Mixed-paid invoice, CN total ABOVE the card portion → type a refund between the card cap and the CN total → inline error names the CARD cap and Issue is disabled while Allocated still reads ✓. *(walked 2026-08-02 on a fixture built for this box — **VLX-000067**, total **$88.59 = $20.00 credits + $68.59 card** (`pi_3U015R…`), no prior CNs. Cap ($68.59) < CN total ($80.00) < invoice total ($88.59), so the three candidate derivations disagree and the observed number identifies which one runs. Baseline first: CN $80 with Credit auto-defaulted to $80 → `Allocated $80.00 / $80.00 ✓`, Issue **enabled** — so any later disable is attributable. Typing **$75** auto-filled Credit to $5.00, Allocated **stayed** `$80.00 / $80.00 ✓`, and the error read **"Refund cannot exceed $68.59 paid via card."** with Issue **disabled** — allocation satisfied and Issue still blocked, so the card cap is the SOLE failing gate. Boundary is exact to the cent: $68.59 clears the error and re-enables Issue, $68.60 re-raises it. Server twin refuses the same allocation with 422 "refund amount (75.00) exceeds payment-method refundable (68.59) — 68.59 paid via card". Supersedes the 2026-07-30 attempt on VLX-000052, where the card portion WAS the full total, so it could not distinguish a cap wired to the invoice total.)*
- [x] Same invoice after a CN has already refunded the card portion → cap reads $0.00 and the error names the prior refunds. *(walked 2026-08-02 on VLX-000053, whose CN-000020 already took its full $62.60: the field label reads **`max $0.00`**, and a $2.00 CN with $1.00 in Refund produced **"Refund cannot exceed $0.00 paid via card (after $62.60 prior refunds)."** with Allocated `$2.00 / $2.00 ✓` and Issue disabled. This is the guard against refunding the same card money twice across notes.)*
- [x] Invoice paid OUTSIDE Stripe (Record payment) → the Refund-to-card channel is unavailable, not merely capped. *(walked 2026-08-02 on **VLX-000068**, $53.62 settled by bank transfer — PI field holds the synthetic `out_of_band:2026-08-02T15:22:52Z` marker. The refund field is **disabled**, its label reads **"not paid by card"** instead of a max, and the hint says "This invoice was paid outside Stripe, so there is no card to refund. Use Credit balance or Outside Stripe." Server refuses the allocation with "this invoice was not paid by card … route the amount to credit_amount_cents or out_of_band_amount_cents", and `POST /v1/invoices/{id}/refund` refuses with "invoice was paid outside Stripe". Credit-balance and out-of-band allocations remain fully available on the same invoice.)*
- [x] Both credit-note ceilings are visible BEFORE typing, and each explains itself. *(walked 2026-08-03 on **VLX-000067**, which by then carried CN-000030 ($80.00 total, $10.00 of it refunded to card). Amount field reads **`max $8.59`** with the subline **"$80.00 of $88.59 already credited by earlier credit notes."**; Refund field reads **`max $58.59`** — $68.59 card portion less the $10.00 already refunded. Typing $80 into Amount raises **"Credit note cannot exceed $8.59 still creditable ($80.00 of $88.59 already credited)."** inline with Issue disabled, instead of the previous behaviour where the Amount field advertised no ceiling at all (`max=999999.99`) and the limit was discoverable ONLY by submitting and being rejected by the API. Both ceilings now count every non-voided note including DRAFTS, matching what the server counts — the page previously fed the dialog an issued-only list, so a draft holding a card refund was invisible to `max` while the server was already subtracting it.)*
- [x] The Refund-to-card ceiling is the BINDING limit, and tracks the credit note total live. *(walked 2026-08-03 on **VLX-000067**, where the two rules disagree: $58.59 of card money is still refundable but only $8.59 of the invoice is still creditable. Before a total is typed the refund field reads **`max $8.59`** — the note's bound, not the card's — with the subline **"Limited by the invoice, not the card — $8.59 is all that is still creditable. The $58.59 left on the card can no longer be refunded."** — it names the binding rule and states plainly that the leftover card money is unreachable, because no future note on this invoice can exceed $8.59 either. Typing **$5.00** into Amount moves the refund ceiling to **`max $5.00`** and the subline to **"Limited by this note's $5.00 total — raise it to refund up to $8.59, all that is still creditable."**, which is honest precisely because raising the note here DOES unlock more — a refund can never exceed the note it belongs to. Previously the field advertised `max $58.59` and accepted $20 on an $8.59 note; nothing stopped it until the allocation line went ✗, so the label was a promise the field did not keep. Negative control on **VLX-000068** (bank transfer): the label still reads **"not paid by card"** and the note-capped subline does NOT appear — with no card at all, the note's size is not what is stopping you.)*
- [x] The sidebar signals that its navigation scrolls, at every window height. *(walked 2026-08-03 at 1440×800, the height a laptop actually runs at: the nav needs 857px in a 628px pane, so **229px sits below the fold** — Audit Log, Webhooks, API Keys, **Settings** and Test Clocks. macOS overlay scrollbars are invisible until you scroll, so the list previously appeared to simply END at "Dunning policies"; an operator screenshot of a second session showed exactly that, with the SYSTEM and TEST MODE sections absent. Nothing was ever broken — the pane scrolls and every item hit-tests as clickable — it gave no reason to try. Now a fade renders at whichever edge has content beyond it: at the top of the scroll **bottom fade only**, mid-scroll **both**, at the bottom **top fade only**, and at 1440×1200 (all 17 items fit) **no fade at all**, reacting to the resize without a reload. The fades are `pointer-events-none`: Settings still hit-tests through the overlay. Both gradients confirmed to paint (`backgroundImage !== none`) rather than silently no-op on a renamed utility class.)*
- [x] Every scrollable content pane signals that it scrolls — enforced, not per-site. *(walked 2026-08-03. Audit found **15** vertical scroll containers; **7 content panes** now route through `<ScrollPane>` (sidebar nav, onboarding steps, webhook event-type picker, recipe items, plan unattached-meters, webhook response body, customer sent-emails). Verified on the sidebar at 1440×800: mask is `linear-gradient(#000 0, #000 calc(100% - 24px), transparent)` at scroll top, both edges mid-scroll, top-only at the bottom, and **absent entirely** at 1440×1200 where all 17 items fit — reacting to resize with no reload. Settings still hit-tests through the mask. A CSS mask on the content, NOT an overlaid gradient, so it cannot mismatch a surface background or intercept clicks. The eslint gate is mutation-verified: adding `<div className="max-h-48 overflow-y-auto">` to a new page fails lint with the message pointing at ScrollPane. **Deferred with reason:** 4 menu primitives (dropdown/select/command/combobox — scrolling a menu is understood, and they carry keyboard nav) and 3 `DialogContent` bodies, which scroll as one box INCLUDING their close button, so masking would fade that button; giving dialogs a scrolling body inside a fixed shell is a redesign of every dialog and is annotated inline at each of the 3 sites.)*
- [x] The content band is 1600px and the top-bar chrome rides the same band. *(walked 2026-08-03 at 2560×1330: content band measures **1600px** at x=600..2200, gutter **360px** each side (was 1280 / 520). Both the page content and the sticky top bar resolve from ONE constant — 2 elements carry `max-w-[1600px]`, and the Test/Live toggle's right edge now lands within **32px** of the content's right edge, matching the band's own `md:px-8` padding. Before this it sat at x=2544 against content ending at 2040 — **504px adrift**, chrome floating free of everything it relates to.)*
- [x] No clipped text on the audit feed is unrecoverable. *(walked 2026-08-03 at 2560×1330: `/audit-log` previously clipped **37** actor cells — a fixed `w-28` (112px) holding values like `owner@walkthrough.local` needing 142px — and the one recovery channel was broken: `title` carried `entry.actor_id` while the cell rendered `formatActorName(entry)`, so hovering a clipped email revealed a **UUID, not the email**. Now **0** clipped, and the tooltip carries both: `owner@walkthrough.local · vlx_usr_8f06c6a6…`. This is the one dense route with no scroll container, so width was the only relief available.)*
- [x] Two API keys are distinguishable in the audit feed. *(walked 2026-08-03. The deleted-key fallback rendered `Key ${actor_id.slice(0,16)}...`; because Velox ids are time-sortable, keys minted in the same window share a long LEADING prefix and differ only at the tail — the slice threw away exactly the distinguishing characters. Verified against real data: `vlx_key_d9d763rm` and `vlx_key_d9ksum3m` each collapsed **two distinct keys** into one identical string. Middle-truncation now renders `Key vlx_key_d9…maoqif750` vs `…maoqif75g` — distinguishable. Fires precisely in the forensic case, where the key was deleted so the name join misses and the id is all that remains.)*
- [x] A clipped tooltip shows what the cell shows. *(walked 2026-08-03 on `/credit-notes`: the Reason cell renders `creditNoteReasonLabel(note.reason)` — e.g. "Quantity decrease" — while its `title` carried the raw enum `subscription_quantity_decrease`. Hover now matches the cell. For operator free-text reasons the two strings are identical, so nothing is lost either way.)*
- [x] The sent-emails list can recover a clipped recipient or failure reason. *(walked 2026-08-03 on `/customers/:id`: both `to {recipient}` and the red `last_error` line are single-line `truncate` with **no** `title` and neither string restated anywhere else on the page — zero recovery channels. Both now carry one. The failure reason is the operator's only account of why a billing email did not arrive.)*
